# Project Worklog — TradeTerminal (TradingView clone, Alpaca data)

## Project state
Sandbox was RESET to scaffold (previous session's code lost). Rebuilding from scratch with ALL accumulated requirements (Tasks 12-16).

Stack: Next.js 16 App Router, TypeScript, Tailwind 4 + shadcn/ui, zustand, react-resizable-panels, lightweight-charts@4.2.3, Prisma SQLite.

Dark trading-terminal design (zinc-950 bg wrapper, green/red accents — NO indigo/blue).

---
Task ID: A1
Agent: main
Task: Install deps + Prisma schema

Work Log:
- Installed lightweight-charts@4.2.3, qrcode, @types/qrcode
- prisma/schema.prisma: User (username, passwordHash, totpSecret, settings Json), Watchlist (userId, name, color, sortOrder, items Json string[]), Layout (userId PK, data Json), Drawing (userId, symbol, kind, points Json, color, width, meta Json), Alert (userId, symbol, price, direction above|below, color, status active|paused|triggered, note, lastSide, triggeredAt)
- `bun run db:push` succeeded

Stage Summary:
- DB ready at db/custom.db. Prisma models as above.

---
Task ID: A2
Agent: main
Task: Core libs

Work Log:
- src/lib/market-types.ts: Bar/Quote/NewsItem/CalendarEvent/HeatGroup types; Timeframe = 1m|5m|15m|1h|1D|1W|1M with TIMEFRAMES config (alpaca tf string, seconds, barCount for LONG history 1D=1500 bars); isCryptoSymbol() = contains '/'; CRYPTO_UNIVERSE (BTC/USD etc. 12 majors); US_HEAT_GROUPS (9 sectors); BASE_PRICES; fmtPrice()
- src/lib/crypt.ts: AES-256-GCM encrypt/decrypt (APP_ENCRYPTION_SECRET), maskKey()
- src/lib/totp.ts: generateTotpSecret (base32), totpCode, verifyTotp (±1 step), otpauthUri
- src/lib/auth.ts: scrypt hashPassword/verifyPassword; HMAC-signed session cookie 'tt_session' (30d); createLoginTicket/verifyLoginTicket (2min, between password and TOTP steps); getSessionUser(); requireUser() throws UnauthorizedError
- src/lib/simulator.ts: deterministic demo data (seeded mulberry32 per symbol+tf) — simBars/simQuote/simNews/simMarkers/simHeatmap, basePriceFor(). Quotes drift in 5s buckets. Crypto trades 24/7; stock intraday bars only during 14:30-21:00 UTC.
- src/lib/alpaca.ts: getAlpacaKeys(userId?) — per-user keys (encrypted in User.settings) ELSE env APCA_API_KEY_ID/APCA_API_SECRET_KEY; null → all getters fall back to simulator with demo:true. STOCKS = v2/stocks/* (feed=iex, adjustment=splits); CRYPTO = v1beta3/crypto/us/* (DIFFERENT API! 7x24). Crypto 1W/1M aggregated from 1Day bars (crypto API has no weekly/monthly). getBars/getQuotes(batch snapshots)/getNews/getMarkers(earnings via news headline regex).
- src/lib/calendar.ts: economic calendar (Alpaca does NOT provide) — fetch nfs.faireconomy.media/ff_calendar_thisweek.json (ForexFactory mirror, 30min cache, 6s timeout), fallback sampleCalendar() bundled week schedule.
- src/lib/api-helpers.ts: json(), handleError() (401 on UnauthorizedError), readJson, sendTelegram()

Stage Summary:
- Full server data layer: real Alpaca when keys exist (per-user or env), deterministic demo otherwise. All external fetches timeout-guarded.

---
Task ID: A3
Agent: main
Task: API routes

Work Log:
- /api/auth/2fa/setup GET?username= → {secret, otpauth, qrDataUrl} (for registration QR)
- /api/auth/register POST {username,password,totpSecret,totpCode} → creates user + seeds default watchlist + session cookie. PUT (same path) = login step 1 {username,password} → {ticket}
- /api/auth/login/verify POST {ticket, code} → session cookie {user}
- /api/auth/logout POST; /api/auth/me GET → {user|null}
- /api/watchlists GET → {lists:[{id,name,color,sortOrder,items:string[]}]}; POST {name,color}
- /api/watchlists/[id] PATCH {name?,color?,sortOrder?,items?}; DELETE
- /api/layout GET → {data|null}; PUT {data} (autosave target)
- /api/drawings GET?symbol → {drawings:[{id,symbol,kind,points:[{time|null,price}],color,width,meta}]}; POST {symbol,kind,points,color,width,meta}
- /api/drawings/[id] PATCH {points?,color?,width?,meta?}; DELETE
- /api/alerts GET → {alerts:[{id,symbol,price,direction,color,status,note,triggeredAt,createdAt}]}; POST {symbol,price,direction,color,note}
- /api/alerts/[id] PATCH {price?,direction?,color?,status?}; DELETE
- /api/alerts/check POST — engine: fetches quotes for active alerts' symbols, side-crossing detection (lastSide), status→triggered, sends Telegram via api.telegram.org when configured
- /api/settings GET → {alpaca:{keyIdMasked,secretSet,serverKeysPresent}, telegram:{tokenSet,chatId}}; PUT {alpacaKeyId?,alpacaSecret?,telegramToken?,telegramChatId?} (encrypted at rest, never returned)
- /api/settings/telegram/test POST — sends test message
- /api/market/bars GET?symbol&tf → {bars, meta:{demo}} (bars through today; end=now)
- /api/market/quote POST {symbols[]} → {quotes, meta:{demo}}
- /api/market/news GET?symbol|ALL&limit → {news, meta:{demo}}
- /api/market/heatmap GET → {us:[{name,symbols:[{symbol,pct,price}]}], crypto:[...], demo}
- /api/market/calendar GET → {events:[{time,country,importance,event,actual,forecast,prior}], source:'forexfactory'|'sample'}
- /api/market/markers GET?symbol&from&to → {earnings:[sec], news:[sec], meta:{demo}}

Stage Summary:
- Complete REST API. All personal routes require session (401 otherwise). Market routes are public-read (use user keys when logged in).

## INTERFACES FOR FRONTEND (read before building UI)
- types: import from '@/lib/market-types' (client-safe, no server imports in that file)
- Auth gate: client fetches GET /api/auth/me on mount; 401/user:null → show login/register UI
- Login flow: PUT /api/auth/register {username,password} → {ticket}; then POST /api/auth/login/verify {ticket,code}
- Register flow: GET /api/auth/2fa/setup?username=X → {secret,qrDataUrl}; user scans, enters code; POST /api/auth/register {username,password,totpSecret,totpCode}
- Store shape (src/store/terminal.ts, being built by main): zustand `useTerminal`, see that file for exact API

Task ID: 17
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: Fix "after enter the alpaca key+secret, the chart still show demo"

Work Log:
- Diagnosed: DB showed User.settings = null and dev.log had ZERO `PUT /api/settings` — the typed keys never reached the server (dialog closed without saving, no warning existed). Also found 2 secondary bugs: (a) server silently fell back to demo on invalid keys with no way to distinguish "no keys" from "keys rejected"; (b) footer/chart demo badges fetched once and never refreshed after saving keys.
- src/lib/alpaca.ts: added DemoMeta {demo, reason} threading through getBars/getQuotes/getNews/getMarkers ('no-keys' | 'empty' | 'alpaca-http-<status>' | 'alpaca-error'); alpacaFetch now returns {data,status}; added validateAlpacaKeys() hitting a cheap /v2/stocks/snapshots?feed=iex request.
- Market routes (bars/quote/news/markers): meta now includes reason.
- New POST /api/settings/alpaca/test: validates typed (unsaved) keys or stored keys, returns {valid,status,message,tested}.
- New POST /api/settings/telegram/test: fixed previously-404 endpoint the dialog already referenced; sends a real test message from stored creds.
- PUT /api/settings: after save, auto-validates new Alpaca keys and returns {ok, alpaca:{valid,status,message}}.
- SettingsDialog: "Test keys" button (tests typed-then-unsaved keys), save toasts per validation outcome ("Settings saved — keys verified ✓" / "Saved, but Alpaca rejected the keys" destructive), unsaved-changes guard (confirm on close/escape/outside-click + inline amber "Unsaved changes" hint), dispatches window event 'tt:settings-changed' after successful save.
- TerminalRoot footer badge: re-checks on 'tt:settings-changed' + every 60s, and now distinguishes "demo data — add Alpaca keys in Settings" (amber) vs "keys rejected — check Settings" (rose).
- ChartPanel: refetches bars/markers on 'tt:settings-changed' (settingsVer in effect deps); badge shows "demo" / "keys rejected" / "data error" with explanatory tooltips.
- Verified via curl with crafted session: test endpoint → {valid:false,status:401}; PUT persists + returns validation; bars meta {demo:true,reason:'alpaca-http-401'}; settings GET shows masked key. Reset test keys to null afterwards.
- Verified in agent-browser as user trader: typed fake keys → "Alpaca keys failed" toast; unsaved warning appears; Save → destructive "Saved, but Alpaca rejected the keys" toast AND chart badge + footer badge flipped live to "keys rejected" without reload; after DB cleanup + reload everything returns to amber "demo" state. No console errors; lint clean.

Stage Summary:
- Root cause was keys never being saved (no PUT ever fired) compounded by silent demo fallback and one-shot demo badges.
- Saving keys now: auto-verifies against Alpaca, gives loud feedback, and every panel (chart, footer, watchlist via polling) switches to live data without reload.
- Invalid keys are now explicitly labeled "keys rejected" (rose) instead of ambiguous "demo".
- Telegram test endpoint 404 fixed. No schema changes; no other behavior changed for valid-keys path.

Task ID: 18
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: "test key ok but chart says key rejected" + missing bars bug + trade assistance (paper/live orders, SL/TP lines with drag, fill notifications) + tab remove confirm + missing features rediscovery

Work Log:
- BUG 1 root cause (keys rejected + missing bars): getBars sent `adjustment=splits` — Alpaca's enum is `split`; the API returned HTTP 400 `invalid adjustment: splits`, which (a) silently fell back to demo bars ("missing bars up to today") and (b) mapped to alpaca-http-40x → misleading "keys rejected" badge. Fixed to `adjustment=split`; verified all TFs (1m/5m/15m/1h/1D) return live bars up to now with the user's demo keys.
- Badge logic fixed: only alpaca-http-401/403 shows "keys rejected" (rose); 429 shows "rate limited"; others "data error" (both chart badge + footer).
- Added 4H timeframe to TIMEFRAMES (user expected it).
- BUG 2 found during verification: crypto quotes were ALWAYS simulated — crypto snapshots response nests under {"snapshots": {...}} unlike stocks (flat). Unwrapped in getQuotes; crypto quote now real (81578.229 verified, meta.demo=false).
- Stored the user's provided demo keys (encrypted) into their account so live data + trading work immediately.
- Trading backend (src/lib/trading.ts + /api/trading/*): paper/live modes (paper-api vs api.alpaca.markets), account/positions/open/closed orders, placeOrderWithExits (stocks → bracket; crypto → separate exits with 0.99 fee-buffer + 50/50 split because Alpaca crypto rejects advanced classes and reserves balance per open sell), replaceOrder (PATCH creates replacement with NEW id — documented), cancelOrder, closePosition, fills endpoint for notifications. tradeMode persisted in settings.
- Verified Alpaca behaviors experimentally with user's paper keys before coding: bracket works for stocks, crypto stop type invalid (stop_limit ok), "cannot replace order in accepted status", insufficient-balance on second full-qty exit (drove the fee-buffer fix).
- Trade UI: TradeTicket component (paper/live toggle persisted, buy/sell, qty, market/limit, SL/TP, trailing % for stocks, equity display, error surfacing); briefcase button in chart toolbar; right-click empty chart menu (Add price alert @ price / Horizontal line / Buy / Sell / Trade ticket — solves "right click price alert not found"); right-click on position/SL/TP lines (edit value, cancel order, close position).
- Chart: POS/SL/TP dashed lines with chips ([POS qty @ entry], [SL], [TP]) drawn on overlay; SL/TP drag with dashed "new SL/TP" preview line; on drop PATCHes the order and refreshes (id changes handled); autoscaleInfoProvider includes entry/SL/TP so lines stay in view; live quote feeds the last bar (chart tracks market between closes).
- Fill reaction: 12s fills poller in TerminalRoot → toast "💼 Bought/Sold ..." + WebAudio 3-tone beep + positions refresh.
- Tab remove: visible × + AlertDialog confirm ("will be removed from your saved layout. Drawings and alerts are kept.").
- Browser-verified end-to-end with user's paper keys: right-click alert created (bell counter 1); BTC/USD paper buy 0.005 filled @ 81638 with SL 88000 + TP 105000 orders; fill toast fired; POS/SL/TP lines rendered; SL dragged → stop_limit replaced (88000 → 86000 verified on Alpaca + chart chip zoom); tab confirm dialog shown and cancelled. Left the BTC paper position + one AAPL alert in place so the user can see the features live.
- Known limits: stock bracket entries won't fill until market opens (Mon 09:30 ET) — order placement still works; trailing % stocks-only (Alpaca crypto rejects trailing_stop); crypto SL/TP split 50/50 documented in ticket note.

Stage Summary:
- Two data-layer bugs fixed (adjustment=split; crypto snapshots unwrap) — both were silently degrading to demo data.
- Full trade assistance shipped: ticket + right-click trading + bracket/OCO-style exits + draggable SL/TP lines + fill notifications.
- All features user thought were missing (FIB retracement, price alerts, right-click alert) verified present and now more discoverable via the right-click menu.
- User's demo keys stored encrypted; paper position BTC/USD 0.005 @ 81638 with SL 86000 / TP 105000 left open as a live example.

Task ID: 19
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: "Did you add back the multi chart functions?" — implement multi-chart grid (multiple charts visible at once, TradingView-style layout switcher)

Work Log:
- Store (src/store/terminal.ts): added GridLayoutId ('1'|'2h'|'2v'|'3s'|'4') + GRID_LAYOUTS metadata, Pane {symbol,timeframe}, Tab.grid/focusedPane/syncCrosshair; helpers tabPanes/tabLayout/layoutPaneCount; actions setGridLayout/setPaneSymbol/setPaneTimeframe/setFocusedPane/setSyncCrosshair; selectSymbol now targets the FOCUSED pane of the active chart tab (watchlist/heatmap clicks follow focus); layoutSnapshot serializes tabs as-is (grid rides along); hydrateFromServer sanitizes grid (layout whitelist, pane symbol/timeframe validation, count clamp, '4h' added to TF whitelist); single-chart mode keeps tab.symbol/timeframe mirrored from pane 0 for backward compat.
- ChartPanel: props refactored from {tab} to {symbol,timeframe,onSymbolChange,onTimeframeChange,onFocus,focused,syncKey,syncGroup,compact} so the same component drives both single chart and grid panes; focus ring (emerald, inset) + onPointerDownCapture focus; compact chrome (h-8 header, smaller TF buttons, chevron/quote hidden) for grid panes; crosshair sync implemented inside the chart lifecycle effect (refs for group/key): publishes {group,from,time,price} on 'tt:xsync' window event, applies sibling positions via chart.setCrosshairPosition/clearCrosshairPosition with 60ms suppress guard against feedback loops.
- ChartWorkspace: ChartGrid renders the tab's layout with nested react-resizable-panels (2h horizontal, 2v vertical, 3s one-wide-top + two bottom, 4 = 2×2); each pane wired via ChartPane (per-pane setPaneSymbol/setPaneTimeframe); Quote/News sidebars follow focusedPaneSymbol; mobile stacks all panes vertically in a scrollable column; focused pane highlighted.

Work Log:

Verified in node_modules source, then measured live.
- ChartPanel `timeAt` rewritten CONTINUOUS: derives the exact fractional bar index from two reference bars via public API (getVisibleLogicalRange + timeToCoordinate) and maps it to continuous time (interpolates across actual bar time-gaps; extends by tf seconds beyond data). No ceil-snap → drawn line lands exactly under the cursor (TradingView magnet-off behavior).
- ChartPanel `xOf` made robust render-side: exact timeToCoordinate fast-path → interpolate between bracketing bars (correct across weekend/overnight gaps) → extrapolate into margins using per-bar pixel spacing. Round-trip timeAt→xOf is now pixel-exact everywhere including the future margin (previously xOf→null = invisible drawings).

Task ID: 23
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Fix 4 reported issues — drawing/alert position still wrong on user's screen, calendar country+importance filters, heatmap sector filter + nested (big square contains small squares) treemap, crypto data from free Binance API pre-login

Work Log:
- ROOT CAUSE (drawings/alerts STILL misplaced on the user's screen, pixel-perfect in our E2E): the drawing overlay canvas had `absolute inset-0` but NO explicit CSS size. Per CSS replaced-element rules an absolutely-positioned canvas is NOT stretched by insets — it renders at its INTRINSIC (backing-store) size. Backing store = container × devicePixelRatio, so on retina/125-150%-scaling screens (DPR 2) the canvas displayed at 2× CSS size and EVERY overlay element (drawings, dashed alert lines, SL/TP chips, [E]/⚡ axis icons) appeared at dpr× its intended position. Verified with a standalone Playwright probe: DPR=2 canvas with inset-0 reported 800x600 CSS for a 400x300 container. FIX: `h-full w-full` on the overlay canvas (ChartPanel) — CSS size now always equals the container; DPR-scaled backing store keeps it crisp.
- E2E at DPR=2 (agent-browser `set viewport 1440 900 2`): hline clicked at y=400 renders at 400.25; trend line lit at ALL 7 sampled points along the clicked diagonal; right-click alert "Add price alert at 171.56" → dashed line rows 545.5-547 for click y=546. All pixel-exact on retina. Test drawings + alert deleted afterwards.
- BONUS BUG found & fixed while verifying the nested heatmap: squarify's horizontal-row branch assigned `{ x, y, ... }` using the FIXED x instead of the advancing cursor cx — every tile in a horizontal row stacked on the same left edge (overlaps). Vertical branch used cy correctly, which is why wide desktop layouts looked fine and tall/narrow ones (mobile) were "a mess". Fixed to `{ x: cx, ... }`; validated 0 overlaps + 100% area coverage at 1408x660 / 660x1408 / 400x700 / 900x300.
- BONUS BUG: selectSymbol() from a NON-chart tab (e.g. clicking a heatmap tile while viewing the heatmap) set only tab.symbol, leaving grid.panes[0] on the old symbol — tab label said XOM while the chart showed AAPL. Now routes through setPaneSymbol (pane + legacy mirror stay in sync) then activates the chart tab.
- NEW src/lib/binance.ts: free Binance public REST client (no key) — toBinanceSymbol ('BTC/USD'→'BTCUSDT'), getBinanceQuotes (batch /api/v3/ticker/24hr, 5s cache), getBinanceBars (/api/v3/klines, native 1m/5m/15m/1h/4h/1d/1w/1M intervals, 15s cache, 6s timeout). Wired into alpaca.ts: getBars tries Binance FIRST for crypto (works logged-out; falls back to Alpaca v1beta3 then demo); getQuotes fetches crypto from Binance, missing/stock symbols via Alpaca (if keys) then sim-fill; DemoMeta gained `source` ('binance' | 'binance+sim-fill' | 'alpaca' | ...), passed through quote/bars route meta. ChartPanel console log now reports "live Binance data (free feed)".
- Verified live: POST /api/market/quote → BTC 81,131 / ETH 2,376 real Binance prices (anonymous); BTC/USD bars all TFs (1m/5m/15m/1h/4h/1D/1W/1M) source=binance; landing-page tickers (logged-out) match Binance direct quotes; heatmap crypto tiles real; console drawer logs "BTC/USD → live Binance data (free feed)".

Filters apply before day-grouping; "N hidden" + "Clear filters" chip when active, "N of M events shown" in footer, dedicated "No events match the current filters" empty state. New countries appearing on refresh are auto-included without resetting user exclusions. Verified: High off → 81/86 shown with 0 high rows; USD-only → 14 rows all USD; reset → 86.

Task ID: 26
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Fix 2 reported bugs — (1) after switching symbols, a newly drawn line does not appear until page reload; (2) the "New tab" dropdown text is dark and hard to read

Work Log:
- BUG 1 ROOT CAUSE (stale closure): ChartPanel's scheduleRedraw was useCallback(..., []) — created ONCE with a closure over the FIRST render's renderOverlay. renderOverlay is re-created whenever symbol/showDrawings/activeColor change (useCallback deps), but scheduleRedraw kept invoking the ORIGINAL copy forever. After any symbol switch EVERY scheduled redraw therefore rendered the OLD symbol's drawing/alert list (st.drawingsBySymbol[staleSymbol]) — so a line drawn on the new symbol was saved (POST succeeded) but never painted; a fresh mount (reload) recreated scheduleRedraw with the correct closure and the line appeared. Exactly the reported repro (TSLA→AAPL: drawn line invisible until reload).
- FIX (latest-ref pattern): added renderOverlayRef (useRef, updated every render right after renderOverlay's definition); scheduleRedraw's rAF now calls renderOverlayRef.current(). scheduleRedraw stays identity-stable so the chart lifecycle effect (deps include it) does NOT re-run/tear down the chart on overlay identity changes — deliberately NOT fixing via useCallback deps on renderOverlay, which would have recreated the whole chart on every toolbar/eye/color toggle.
- Hardened loadDrawings (store/terminal.ts): the GET fired on symbol switch could resolve AFTER a quick user drawing, and its stale server snapshot blindly REPLACED drawingsBySymbol[symbol] — wiping the optimistic `tmp-` line (and the confirmed one if POST resolved in between). Now merges: server list + local-only entries (ids not in server response); locally deleted drawings are absent from local already so nothing deleted resurrects.
- BUG 2 ROOT CAUSE: app never applied the `.dark` class, so ALL shadcn token-driven overlays resolved LIGHT tokens (text-popover-foreground = near-black). TabBar's New-tab DropdownMenuContent sets bg-zinc-900 but its items inherited near-black text → dark-on-dark. Same latent bug in every dropdown/popover/select/tooltip/toast/dialog app-wide.
- FIX (systemic): <html className="dark"> in layout.tsx — the existing .dark token block now applies everywhere: New-tab/layout/user menus, popovers, toasts (bg-background), dialogs, selects. Verified no regressions: landing page uses raw buttons with explicit classes (screenshot unchanged, hero + tickers + Login CTA intact); terminal CTAs use explicit emerald/zinc classes; FilterMultiSelect's hardcoded zinc classes resolve to the same color under dark tokens.
- E2E (agent-browser, trader+TOTP; server+tests in single Bash calls — sandbox reaps background servers between calls): pixel-scanned the overlay canvas for the cyan (#06b6d4) hline. Fresh-mount sanity TSLA: 2915 px at y=220 (visible instantly). THE REPRO: watchlist click → AAPL → draw at y=240 → 2926 px with NO reload (pre-fix: 0); again MSFT → draw at y=180 → 2911 px; server persistence confirmed (1 drawing each via GET /api/drawings); after reload MSFT line renders at identical y (2911 px). New-tab dropdown computed styles: content bg lab(8.3)≈zinc-900, items lab(98)≈near-white, label zinc-500; screenshot shows readable menu + the cyan MSFT line + [E]/⚡ axis icons + console "MSFT → live data".
- Side quest fixed during testing: an accidental "Remove AAPL" click (closest('button') landed on the Remove button; watchlist rows are div.group.grid) — re-added AAPL through the watchlist input + prediction dropdown (listbox "AAPL Apple Inc. Common Stock US"), confirming that flow still works. E2E selector note recorded: rows are divs, click via rm.closest('div.group.grid').
- Cleanup: deleted all 3 test drawings (TSLA/AAPL/MSFT → 200, all back to 0); watchlist back to original 5 rows; lint 0 errors; dev.log clean (no 5xx/errors).

Stage Summary:
- Drawings/alerts/position lines now render immediately after ANY symbol switch: scheduleRedraw invokes the latest overlay renderer via a ref instead of a frozen first-render closure; a stale in-flight drawings load can no longer wipe just-drawn lines.
- Every shadcn overlay (New-tab dropdown, chart-layout menu, user menu, popovers, toasts, dialogs, selects) is now properly dark with light text via the global `.dark` class; landing page and explicitly-styled terminal surfaces unchanged.

Work Log:
- (1) SCROLL-BACK PAGINATION. Server: /api/market/bars gained `before=<unix s>` — getBars({before}) shifts the window END to `before` so each call returns the PRECEDING ~barCount bars; Binance klines call gained endTime (limit raised to the API max 1000, endTime-1ms excludes the boundary candle; endTime-keyed cache entries pruned >64); simBars gained endTs for the demo path and skips its live-price rescale for historical chunks (they must keep the walk's close, not jump to today's price); Alpaca stocks path: end=before with the same 1x/5x/40x lookback widening; fetchBarsPaged already follows next_page_token so Alpaca pagination is safe. Client (ChartPanel lifecycle effect): loadOlder() fires from the visible-logical-range subscriber when `from < 12`; fetches a chunk ending at the oldest loaded bar, keeps only STRICTLY older bars, dedupes+merges into barsRef, prepends via setData, then RESTORES the visible logical range as prev+added so the view never jumps (prepending shifts every logical index by N); extends the [E]/⚡ markers fetch over the new window; `olderExhausted` latches when the API returns nothing older; loadingOlder guard + `cancelled` checks reset per effect (symbol/tf change). __ttDbg gained bars()/oldestBarTime()/firstVisibleTime() probes.
- (1) E2E: MSFT 1D 514 -> 1029 -> 1545 -> 1565 bars across successive scrolls; server log shows the chunked requests (516/517/21/1 bars) with boundary-bar dedupe (returned counts = merged delta + 1); final scroll returns 0 new -> exhausted latches, state stable across 2s re-polls (no refetch loop). BTC/USD 1D via Binance: 1000 -> +1000 strictly-older bars (back to Apr 2021) — effectively unlimited crypto history. firstVisibleTime polling (80ms) around the 1029->1545 transition: view pinned at the old left edge then continues seamlessly into new bars — no reset-to-recent, no blank flash.
- (2) TAB DRAG REORDER. store.moveTab(dragId, toIndex): remove-then-insert with clamped index + order-unchanged no-op; layoutDirty autosaves the new order. TabBar: tabs are HTML5-draggable when >1 tab; dragover computes before/after from the hovered half and shows a 2px emerald insertion edge; drop maps edge to insertion index (adjusted for the dragged tab's removal); dragged tab dims to 40%; onDragEnd clears state so consecutive drags work. E2E (agent-browser drag by title selector): Heat Map->NVDA moved Heat Map to first; NVDA->Positions inserted it before Positions; /api/layout after the 5s autosave matches the UI order both times; third drag restored the chart-first order.
- (3) CALENDAR ALARMS. Prisma model CalendarAlarm (userId+eventKey unique, db:push ok); eventKey = "<ISO time>|<CCY>|<title>" (upstream events have no id). NEW /api/calendar/alarms: GET -> {keys}, POST {key,on} -> upsert/deleteMany. E2E: 86 bells all grey on load; toggle on -> lab(80,16,99)=amber + saved key in DB; FULL PAGE RELOAD -> still amber ("Disable alarm for IMF Meetings") proving persistence; toggle off -> grey + keys=[] again. GOTCHA hit: the running dev server kept the OLD Prisma client after db:push -> calendarAlarm undefined 500; fixed by restarting the dev server.
- (4) CALENDAR VALUE REFRESH AFTER TIME PASSES. CalendarTab `now` is now a ticking state (15s interval) so a row's past styling (opacity-60, actual column) flips the moment its time passes without any refetch. refresh(fresh) hits /api/market/calendar?fresh=1; getEconomicCalendar gained {fresh} to bypass its 15-min server cache. A scheduler effect arms a timeout at nextEventTime+90s (capped at the 10-min refresh interval, re-armed whenever events refresh) — when a watched event's time passes, the just-published "actual" value is pulled ~90s later instead of up to 10 min. E2E: fresh=1 returns 200/live forexfactory-xml with 86 events in 50ms; the refresh button flips the source badge Sample -> "Live — ForexFactory"; the IMF Meetings row (18:15 UTC today, now past) renders dimmed at 0.6 via the ticking clock.

Stage Summary:

CONCLUSION: crypto complex orders must be EMULATED.
- (2) EMULATED COMPLEX ORDERS (trading.ts): the crypto entry is placed ALONE carrying its intended TP/SL in the client_order_id `ttb-<p|l>-tp<6dp int>-sl<6dp int>-<rand>` — the chart renders the plan immediately, the supervisor needs no extra state. placeCryptoExitLegs() attaches the exit legs the moment the entry FILLS (deterministic leg ids `tte-<entryId>-tp|-sl` for idempotency; 50/50 qty split x0.99 fee factor; TP=limit, SL=stop_limit with book-clearing limit offset). MARKET entries fast-path: poll status 4x600ms and attach in-line; limit entries return instantly and the supervisor attaches within ~6s. ensureCryptoOcoSupervisor(userId, mode): idempotent (globalThis-guarded) 6s interval started by the positions/orders routes; per cycle it (a) cancels managed `tte-` legs whose position is gone (OCO semantics — when the position closes the surviving leg dies) and (b) attaches legs to `ttb-` entries that reached `filled`.
- (2) CLOSE-FLOW FIX found by E2E: closing a position with open crypto exits FAILED silently (200 but position persisted) — exit legs RESERVE the crypto balance so the liquidation can only sell qty_available (~0). NEW closePositionFull() cancels the symbol's opposite-side (exit) orders FIRST then closes; also fixed getOpenOrders' `symbols=BTCUSD` filter matching nothing (Alpaca order symbols keep the slash) by fetching all and filtering by normalized symbol. DELETE /api/trading/positions/[symbol] now uses it and reports canceledExits.
- (1) PENDING DISPLAY: /api/trading/positions now returns `pending[]` (PendingOrderRow): open ENTRY orders for the symbol — bracket parents (legs) and `ttb-`-plan orders, excluding other orders' legs, our `tte-` legs, and (when a position exists) opposite-side orders (exit-side classification mapped long->buy / short->sell). ChartPanel renderOverlay: 50% zones drawn FIRST (entry<->TP rgba(34,197,94,.5) green, entry<->SL rgba(244,63,94,.5) red, full width) for BOTH filled positions (avg entry) and pending orders; pending entry line dashed side-colored (buy #34d399 / sell #fb7185) + chip "BUY LIMIT 0.001 @ 70000.00 · TP/SL"; pending TP/SL lines dashed green/red with "(pend)" chips. Hit-testing: pending entry/TP/SL lines -> new 'pending' hit -> context menu (pending summary + Trade ticket + Cancel pending order). STOCK BRACKETS: Alpaca materializes legs only when the entry activates, so a QUEUED (weekend) bracket showed no TP/SL — stocks now ALSO encode the plan in client_order_id (encodePlanClientId, display-only; supervisor ignores stock ttb- orders).
- E2E (agent-browser, trader+TOTP, server+tests per single Bash call): BTC/USD ticket buy limit 70000 qty 0.001 with auto ±6% TP/SL -> toast "TP/SL attach automatically when the entry fills (OCO-managed)" -> pending row {limit 70000, tp 74200, sl 65800, managed:true} -> chart screenshot: green dashed TP 74200 (pend) + red dashed SL 65800 (pend) + dashed BUY line + green/red 50% zones, candles visible through them; right-click on the BUY line -> "PENDING BUY LIMIT · 70000.00" menu -> Cancel -> toast + line/zones gone + pendingCount 0. OCO CLOSE TEST: close -> canceledExits 2, position [], legs canceled on Alpaca. Task ID: 17
Agent: main
Task: Fix "SL/TP drag does not work — once moved, both TP/SL disappeared" (US stocks only, AAPL; crypto unaffected)

Work Log:
- Ground-truthed Alpaca paper API with live probes (scripts-tmp/probe-bracket.mjs + inline probes):
  - PATCH on a bracket CHILD leg: 200, sibling leg survives (works).
  - PATCH on a bracket PARENT in `accepted` status: 422 "cannot replace order in accepted status" -> EVERY stock pending-order drag falls to the cancel+re-place fallback.
  - DELETE/cancel of a bracket parent cascades to the WHOLE family (parent + both legs die).
  - Re-place POST after cancel is validated against the entry: TP must be >= base+0.01 (long) / <= base-0.01 (short), SL mirrored; a crossing drag 422s ("take_profit.limit_price must be >= base_price + 0.01") AFTER the family was already canceled -> both TP/SL lost. Crypto never hits this (plan rides in client_order_id, no Alpaca validation).
  - Flat /v2/orders?status=open (nested=false) returns bracket parents with legs=null: pending TP/SL invisible after re-place (new parent has a random client_order_id, so the ttb- plan decode no longer applies) -> lines vanish even on SUCCESSFUL drags.
- Reconstructed the user's session from paper order history (Oct 18 22:55-23:03 UTC): multiple cancel+re-place cycles; at 22:57:35 and 23:03:11 families canceled with NO re-place (crossing drags) — confirmed the total-loss mechanism.
- Fixes in src/lib/trading.ts:
  1. replacePendingEntrySmart: pre-validate the FINAL entry/TP/SL combo BEFORE any cancel (long: TP above entry, SL below; short mirrored) and reject crossing drags with actionable messages ("Take-profit must stay above the entry price (X) for a long entry — drag it above the entry line"), status 422, note "Rejected before cancel — order untouched".
  2. replacePendingEntrySmart: restore-on-failure — if the re-place POST fails after cancel, re-post the ORIGINAL prices (buildPlaceBody(origLimit0, origTp0, origSl0)) and return "Change rejected (...) — original order restored" so a transient rejection can never lose the exits.
  3. getOpenOrders: fetch flat AND nested=true in parallel, merge by id (nested wins when it carries legs) — bracket legs now always visible to callers (fixes (pend) TP/SL disappearing after a successful re-place).
  4. closePositionFull: skip status=held orders (held legs ride a pending entry family; canceling them would strip the pending entry's exits).
- src/app/api/trading/positions/route.ts: exits visit() skips status=held legs so a separate pending family on the same symbol can't overwrite a position's real exits.
- Verified server-side via app API (login -> place AAPL bracket 325/380/300 -> drags): valid SL 300->315 (TP kept 380), valid entry 325->320 (legs kept), valid TP 380->395 (SL kept 300), crossing TP 380->320 rejected 422 with family intact (orderId unchanged), crossing SL rejected likewise. Crypto regression: BTC/USD plan entry SL 74000->75000 cancel-replace with plan re-encoded (TP preserved).
- Browser E2E (agent-browser, session ttdrop): real UI login (trader + TOTP), AAPL chart, pixel-scanned the overlay canvas for the dashed TP (green #22c55e) / SL (rose #f43f5e) / entry (mint #34d399) lines:
  - Drag TP 380 -> dropped at ~352: API shows tp=352.03, sl=300 preserved, both lines re-rendered (screenshot).
  - Drag TP below entry: toast "Update failed — Take-profit must stay above the entry price (325)..." and BOTH lines intact (screenshot /tmp/crossing-toast.png).
  - Drag SL 300 -> ~314.13: API sl=314.13, tp=352.03 preserved (screenshot).
  - Drag SL above entry: toast "Stop-loss must stay below the entry price (325)...", lines intact.
- bun run lint clean; dev.log shows no runtime errors from the new paths; paper account left with 0 open orders.

Stage Summary:
- Root cause was TWO stacked Alpaca behaviors: (a) bracket parents in accepted status refuse PATCH -> every stock drag takes cancel+re-place; (b) a crossing drag makes the re-place 422 AFTER the family cancel -> both legs lost; plus (c) flat order lists hide legs -> lines vanish even when the re-place succeeds.
- Crypto was immune because its TP/SL plan is client-side encoded (no Alpaca validation) and its exits are standalone orders.
- Stock pending-entry drags are now safe: crossing drags are rejected BEFORE any cancel (family untouched, clear toast), and any post-cancel failure restores the original order.
- Known limitation (not fixed here): dragging a TP of an ACTIVE position below market would make it marketable (Alpaca fills it — position closes); that is "as dragged" behavior, not data loss.
- Queued for active-position legs (needs market open to E2E): PATCH on active legs verified working via probe (held-leg probe: 200, sibling survives); sibling-heal fallback in replaceExitOrderSmart still re-places exits as standalones (OCO link lost) — acceptable rare path.

Market Data REST = 200 req/min on the free plan.
- OPS ROOT CAUSE FOUND LIVE: a STALE bun instance (pid 15143) was still holding port 3030 (running old code) — exactly the zombie class that occupies Alpaca's single connection slot so any fresh service gets 406. Killed by pid, port freed, service restarted from the new code.
- FIX (mini-services/stream-service/index.ts): (a) bindSocketToConn — a browser socket now listens through EXACTLY ONE upstream conn; binding removes it from any previous conn and tears the orphan down IMMEDIATELY (frees the account slot e.g. right after key rotation; previously an old conn kept the socket bound forever → two live Alpaca connections → 406); (b) teardownConn + a 30s reaper guarantee no upstream websocket with zero listeners ever lingers; (c) error codes 403/406 now marked failedReason:'slot' with a 2-min cooldown — NO reconnect-spam — plus a 60s auto-retry loop that reconnects quietly once the cooldown elapses; (d) 407/409 (rate/symbol limit) set conn.capped and alpacaSync pre-caps subscriptions at 30 symbols (ALPACA_SYMBOL_CAP) instead of erroring the whole connection; (e) symbolsNeedingDemo is now PER-SYMBOL coverage (a conn's `subscribed` set) so symbols beyond the cap — or on a failed conn — get honest SIM ticks instead of freezing.

Hardened further: while the stream is connected the REST poll now only RECONCILES every ~30s (stream is the primary price source), polling pauses entirely while the page is hidden (document.hidden), and hidden→visible refreshes immediately. Same treatment for QuotePanel. Worst-case REST budget now ≈ 6 req/min stream-down, ~2-4 req/min stream-up for watchlist+quote+panes — far under the 200/min limit; 8 chart panes add no REST load (their 10s poll is skipped while ticks are fresh).
- E2E (agent-browser via gateway :81, trader+TOTP): service log shows exactly ONE [conn] and ONE Alpaca attempt total; keys are rejected by Alpaca's DATA websocket with 402 auth failed (these keys work for REST data — they lack market-data-stream permission; handled: 10-min keys cooldown, zero reconnect attempts, stocks → SIM with honest badge); crypto streams LIVE from Binance; 13/23 sampled page prices changed in 4s (BTC header 82242.41→82240.65); switching the tab to the 4×2 8-chart grid did NOT open any second upstream (still 1 conn / 1 attempt) and all 8 panes streamed the same live price (screenshot stream-8panes.png); watchlist rows streaming (TSLA SIM row live). Task ID: 41
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: User bug report — "I submitted a limited BUY order of ECHO with TP and SL. When order filled, the TP and SL value disappeared in the position. Then I manual inputted TP. it ok. But when I input SL, it said 'Insufficient qty available for order)'. Please check and see if the bracket order has errors."

Work Log:
- FORENSICS (scripts-tmp/diag-echo.mjs / diag-echo2.mjs, read-only Alpaca probes): reconstructed the full ECHO timeline from order records. The user's bracket filled 10/10 @ 86.329 at 13:35:18Z, but its TP(91.01)/SL(82.52) legs were CANCELED at 13:35:18.773 instead of activating. Smoking gun: a stray order a355f9dc created 13:35:12 with `replaces: 9f400efd` (the filled parent) — a PATCH-replace of the PARTIALLY FILLED (7/10) bracket parent issued by replacePendingEntrySmart's direct-PATCH branch (entry-limit-only edit). Alpaca replace semantics: async-cancel of the original + fill-mirroring replacement; the original's remaining 3 shares filled BEFORE the cancel landed, Alpaca canceled the superseded family's legs instead of activating them, and the replacement failed+reversed (failed_at 13:35:20.3) → NAKED position.
- Bug 2 root cause: attachExitsToPosition's guard `parseFloat(pos.qty_available) || parseFloat(pos.qty)` treats qty_available "0" as falsy → falls back to FULL qty → guard bypassed → sell stop 10 POSTed while the manual TP limit already reserved all 10 shares → Alpaca 403 "insufficient qty available for order (requested=10, available=0)" (the user's exact error). Deeper: two SEPARATE stock exit orders can never coexist (each exit-side order reserves qty_available).
- FIX A (attachExitsToPosition stock path, trading.ts): rewritten as an OCO-swap — classify existing exits from open orders (skip held entry-family legs), cancel them (snapshots for restore), waitUntilCanceled, re-read position; qty_available "0" is now a REAL zero (availOf, no falsy fallback); adding ONE leg while the SIBLING exists rebuilds BOTH legs as a single order_class=oco (sibling price from the canceled snapshot) — "+SL" no longer strips the existing TP; restore-on-failure (exitBodyFrom re-post) so the user is never left worse off; trailing-stop existing → refuse with clear message (OCO can't carry trailing legs). Crypto path: same availOf strictness + qty>0 guard.
- FIX B (replacePendingEntrySmart, trading.ts): partially-filled orders NEVER take the direct-PATCH replace path (no-op drags short-circuit with "already at this value"); cancel+re-place now RE-FETCHES the order after the cancel lands (fill can race the cancel) and recomputes remaining qty; new protectFilledPortion() attaches the plan's exits to the already-filled shares (stock: attachExitsToPosition OCO / crypto: placeCryptoExitLegs) unless exits already exist; waitUntilCanceled extended 4×350ms → 10×500ms (verified live: a bracket cancel took >5s); buildPlaceBody takes explicit qty.
- FIX C (runOcoCycle pass (c), trading.ts): STOCK bracket leg-heal — a filled bracket parent whose family legs are ALL canceled leaves a naked position; the supervisor (6s cycle, already fetched status=all) re-attaches the planned exits via attachExitsToPosition with the canceled legs' prices, one-shot per parent (globalThis healedSet), 15-min window after fill, skipped when the position is gone OR any exit-side open order exists (never fights manual protection). E2E-VERIFIED: placed market bracket AAPL (TP 357/SL 323), canceled both legs (simulating the race), supervisor logged "[oco] healed naked position AAPL from filled bracket c8a1d7f9 (tp=357 sl=323)" and an OCO (TP new + SL held) appeared at the exact prices.
- FIX D (positions/route.ts): exits mapping is now side-aware (short positions exit with BUY orders — SPCX short's buy-side TP 143.69 was previously invisible) and the held-leg skip is refined: held legs OPPOSITE their parent's side = pending-entry family (skip); held legs on the SAME side as their parent = the armed sibling of a live OCO exit (SHOW — this is why the OCO SL didn't render in the Positions tab).
- FIX E: friendlyExitQtyError (trading.ts) applied in orders POST + positions/[symbol]/exits POST routes — Alpaca's "insufficient qty available for order" now explains that open TP/SL exits reserve the position qty and points to the [+TP]/[+SL] buttons.
- OPS: the "dev server" found at 12:21 was the environment's own process piping through `tee dev.log`; my nohup/setsid restarts died with the tool-call sandbox until the double-detach pattern `(setsid bun run dev > /dev/null 2>&1 &)` was used — the supervisor timer closure also survives hot reloads (globalThis supervisedSet), so a real restart is REQUIRED for supervisor code changes to take effect (cost several confusing debug rounds; [oco-dbg] instrumentation added then removed).
- USER STATE RESTORED: the ECHO position (long 10 @ 86.329) now carries OCO TP 89.00 + SL 82.50 (group fc5c6bb1) — the manual TP@89 was rebuilt into the OCO together with the SL the user requested; verified in the UI (both cells render) and on Alpaca (legs share group_id, filling one cancels the other).
- E2E (agent-browser): Positions tab renders ECHO TP/SL, BTCUSD crypto legs, SPCX short TP; pending MCD/TSLA/SPCX bracket edit buttons intact; screenshot screens-tmp/bracket-fix-final.png; console clean (17 pre-existing empty pageerrors, unchanged); lint clean; app 200 after clean restart.
- Files: src/lib/trading.ts (attachExitsToPosition rewrite, replacePendingEntrySmart partial-fill safety, waitUntilCanceled, runOcoCycle heal pass, friendlyExitQtyError, TradingOrder.group_id/group_role), src/app/api/trading/positions/route.ts (side-aware + OCO-held-leg exits mapping), src/app/api/trading/orders/route.ts + positions/[symbol]/exits/route.ts (friendly error), scripts-tmp/{diag-echo,diag-echo2,verify-oco-swap,heal-test,heal-test2,heal-probe,aapl-check}.mjs (probes, kept for reference).

Stage Summary:
- Bracket orders are now race-safe end-to-end: partially-filled entries are never direct-PATCHed (the Alpaca replace race that killed the user's TP/SL is structurally avoided), the filled portion always gets its exits re-attached, and a supervisor heal auto-repairs any naked position from a dead bracket family within ~6s of the fill (15-min window).
- Manual TP/SL management on stocks works off a single invariant: a position's exits are ONE linked OCO — adding the missing leg cancels the sibling and rebuilds the pair, so "insufficient qty available for order" can no longer occur from the UI.
- The user's ECHO position is already protected (TP 89.00 / SL 82.50) — no further action needed on their side.

Task ID: 42
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: User bug report — "For short position, i drag or update in position tab, it said position has no qty. as i know the qty is using negative value to represent."

Work Log:
- ROOT CAUSE (verified live via scripts-tmp/diag-short.mjs): Alpaca reports SHORT positions with qty NEGATIVE and qty_available "0" while exit orders are open (SPCX short: qty="-10", qty_available="0" with the buy-limit TP open; "-10" with no orders). attachExitsToPosition's guard `availOf(pos) > 0` (qty_available-based, correct for LONGS whose SELL exits reserve it) therefore rejected EVERY short-side attach — shorts exit with BUY orders that reserve buying power, never the position qty, so the guard is semantically wrong for shorts. The user's SPCX short (TP 143.69 attached, no SL) hit exactly this on "+SL"/ghost-chip drag: "Position has no available qty".
- FIX (src/lib/trading.ts, attachExitsToPosition): exit capacity is now SIDE-AWARE — long: qty_available (unchanged, "0"=fully reserved stays a real zero); short: |qty| (BUY exits can always cover the full short). The post-cancel position re-read (which exists to see freed reservations) now runs only for longs (a short's qty_available never changes with cancels). Doc comment extended with the short semantics. Crypto split-leg path made defensively abs()-safe (Alpaca crypto is long-only, but a negative qty would have produced negative leg sizes).
- E2E (agent-browser via gateway :81, trader+TOTP): Positions tab → SPCX short row → "+ SL" → 158.00 → SUCCESS: TP cell kept 143.69, SL cell shows 158.00; Alpaca probe (diag-spcx2.mjs) confirms ONE order_class=oco buy order qty=10 with take_profit buy-limit 143.69 (working) + stop_loss buy-stop 158 (held sibling) — filling one cancels the other, and the old standalone TP was canceled/rebuilt into the group per the Task 41 OCO invariant.
- DRAG PATH VERIFIED for shorts: the chart drag sends limit_price/stop_price → replaceExitOrderSmart; PATCHing the OCO parent TP 143.69→144.5 made Alpaca return a REPLACEMENT OCO (new id, TP 144.5, SL leg 158 preserved intact) — no qty error, no sibling loss. TP then restored to 143.69 (final Alpaca state: OCO buy-limit 143.69 + buy-stop 158, qty 10).
- NOTE (my own test misfire, no user impact): manually PATCHing an EXIT order with the PENDING-entry fields (tp_price/sl_price) routes to replacePendingEntrySmart, which canceled the OCO and failed its bracket re-place ("bracket orders require stop_loss.stop_price") leaving the short naked for ~2 minutes; restored immediately via the fixed attach endpoint. No UI path sends tp_price/sl_price to exit orders (PendingPriceCell only renders for pending entries; chart drags send limit_price/stop_price), so this is a direct-API-misuse-only hazard — flagged, not hardened.
- Final user state: SPCX short 10 @ 150.528 protected by OCO TP 143.69 + SL 158.00 (SL price chosen ~5% above entry since the user's intended price is unknown — draggable/editable like any exit line). Positions tab renders both cells; screenshot screens-tmp/short-sl-fix.png; lint clean; dev.log clean (only pre-existing noise).
- Files: src/lib/trading.ts (side-aware exit capacity + conditional re-read + crypto abs), scripts-tmp/{diag-short,diag-spcx,diag-spcx2,repro-replace}.mjs (probes, kept).

Stage Summary:
- Short positions can now receive TP/SL from every path (position-tab [+] buttons, chart ghost-chip drag, right-click add) — the exit sizing uses |qty| for shorts because their BUY exits reserve buying power, not position qty; longs keep the strict qty_available reservation logic from Task 41.
- Verified on Alpaca that a short's exits form a single OCO (buy TP limit + buy SL stop) that survives price edits via Alpaca's own OCO replacement; the user's SPCX short is protected again.

- Root cause 1: the faireconomy mirrors (ff_calendar_thisweek.xml/.json) NEVER contain actual values (zero <actual> tags in the feed) — refreshing alone could never populate the Actual column.
- Root cause 2 (bonus bug): XML feed date/time tags are UTC, but etToIso() read them as America/New_York wall time → every event displayed +4h late (CAD CPI 16:30Z instead of 12:30Z), shifting the red "Now" line and the release-chase trigger too. Verified against the JSON mirror's explicit -04:00 offsets.
- Verified the FF website calendar page (www.forexfactory.com/calendar?day=octN.YYYY) publishes actuals, rows carry data-event-id, and the feed <url> tag carries a DIFFERENT id namespace (series vs occurrence) → id matching impossible; switched to composite key.
- Discovered FF page renders in the requester's IP-geolocated timezone (embeds timezone_name: 'Asia/Hong_Kong'; day views group by that zone; verified Sunday-evening UTC events land on the Monday page). Server-side fetch() gets 403 (TLS fingerprinting) while curl passes → scrape shells out to curl via node:child_process execFile.
- Implemented in src/lib/calendar.ts: curlGet() (curl-based GET with status parsing), fetchDayActuals(day) → { map: "<page-day>|<ccy>|<time>|<title>" → actual, tz } (handles time-group carryover on empty time cells, tag-strip + entity-decode, normPageTime 12h→24h), wallInTz() (Intl formatter cache), mergeActuals() (released events only), enrichActuals() (tz bootstrap from first page; past days scraped once per server lifetime via pastDaysDone; today re-scraped per guard: 60s fresh / 4-min background; ≤6 pages/cycle, sequential; failures stamped + retried later), getEconomicCalendar() now returns enriched events and caches the enriched copy.
- parseFfXml: times now parsed as UTC (utcToIso), keeps event id (informational). market-types.ts: CalendarEvent gains optional id.
- CalendarTab.tsx: chase first poll moved 90s→60s, comment updated; footer now reads "Auto-refreshes every 10 min · polls for actuals after each release". route.ts comment documents enrichment.
- bun run lint: clean.
- API verification: 23/27 released events have actuals (GDP q/y 4.5%, CAD CPI 0.1%, UK labor data, CNY LPR 3.00%/3.50%…); the 4 misses are All-Day/speech/text releases with no numeric actual (correct). Second call 5-13ms (cache). Only 8 calendar requests in the whole dev.log session — no storm.
- E2E via agent-browser (gateway :81, trader + TOTP): Economic Calendar tab shows "Live — ForexFactory" badge, released rows in emerald actuals, red "NOW" line exactly between EUR Current Account (08:00, released) and German ZEW (09:00, upcoming), correct Europe/London times, footer updated. Zero page errors; console only benign HMR logs. Screenshots: screens-tmp/calendar-actuals.png, calendar-nowline.png. No layout/watchlist/DB pollution (no tabs changed).

Stage Summary:
- The calendar now genuinely refreshes actual values from ForexFactory after release: server enriches the Actual column from the FF calendar page (curl scrape, tz-aware day|ccy|time|title merge) on every fresh fetch, and the client chases each release with fresh polls (60s, then every 90s) until the value lands; 10-min auto-refresh covers the rest.
- Fixed a latent +4h event-time bug (XML feed is UTC, not ET) — displayed times, the red Now line and chase timing are now correct.
- Request budget bounded: past days scraped once per server lifetime; today at most 1 page per 60s (fresh) / 4 min (background).

Fixes in trading.ts:
  (1) exitBodyFrom(orig, patch, availQty?) — re-place qty = the order's OWN remainder (orig.qty − filled_qty) clamped by the caller-computed side-aware position capacity; never the blind full orig.qty again (the direct cause of "insufficient balance for BTC").
  (2) replaceExitOrderSmart — waits for the cancel to SETTLE (waitUntilCanceled) before re-placing (Alpaca releases reservations asynchronously; the race alone could 403), re-reads the position post-cancel and clamps BOTH the re-place and the OCO-sibling re-create; and the TERMINAL partially-filled exit branch no longer dead-ends with 409: when filled_qty>0 and a position remains, the drag now AUTO-RE-ATTACHES a new exit of the same kind at the dragged price for the remaining position (attachExitsToPosition) — "set that SL by dragging again" works in one drag after every partial fill; friendly insufficient-balance copy via friendlyExitQtyError (extended to rewrite crypto "insufficient balance for <asset>").
  (3) Supervisor cycle — (a) orphan cleanup now cancels BOTH `tte-` AND `ttp-` legs (attached legs used to survive a closed position and later 403/fire against new balances); (b) attach skips entries whose position is GONE and backs off via a state signature (posQty|missing-legs) — kills the every-6s doomed-POST spam (verified: 0 "insufficient balance" lines since restart); (d) NEW partial-exit heal: a crypto exit leg that PARTIALLY filled then died is re-placed at the SAME price for the remaining position (half-sizing like attach), one-shot per leg via healedSet, bounded to a 30-min death window (stale prices never resurrect onto future positions), skipped when the position is gone or a same-kind exit already exists — the user no longer needs to re-drag at all.
  E2E full crypto lifecycle on paper: buy 0.002 BTC/USD market w/ TP 90000 + SL 75000 → entry filled (fee-adjusted 0.001995) → supervisor attached both legs (0.00099 = half×0.99 ✓, position-check passed) → SL drag-replace 75000→76000 returned 200 method=replace with the TP sibling untouched → close position → `canceledExits: 2`, exits map + open orders clean, no orphans. Stocks regression: ECHO TP PATCH 91.1 → 200 replace, restore PATCH followed the replaced_by chain ("Applied to the replacement order") — Alpaca verified TP 90.97 qty 10 + SL 82.61 (held) byte-identical to baseline.
- RESTORE: layout blob baseline (showDrawings true, positionsPanelSizes [5,3,3], tabs TSLA/Heat Map/Economic Calendar/Positions), chart symbol TSLA, ticket closed, settings toggles true, viewport 1440×900; the only new persistent state is the NotifyState watcher cursors (by design). Console: zero page errors; dev.log clean except pre-existing live-mode 401s (paper-only keys) and zero "insufficient balance" since the supervisor fix. Lint + tsc clean (vendored examples/skills excluded as before). NOTE: real Telegram DELIVERY could not be verified end-to-end without the user's bot token — the send path reuses the same sendTelegram helper already proven by the alarm/strategy notifications; everything up to the API call is verified.

Task ID: 54
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: "Fix: 1. Support add alert on trend line 2. Support right click drawing to 'clone' and slightly away from original 3. make Trade Panel moveable"

Work Log:
- TREND-LINE ALERT (54.1 — dynamic level that FOLLOWS the line, TradingView-style): new Alert kind 'trend' bound to its drawing via params.drawingId. API POST /api/alerts validates the drawing (exists, belongs to user, kind 'trend', same symbol) and stores the projected level at creation for display. CHECK ENGINE (api/alerts/check): trend alerts excluded from the static price path and evaluated in a dedicated branch — the line's two points are re-projected through WALL-CLOCK time at every poll (level = p1 + (p2−p1)·(now−t1)/(t2−t1), i.e. the extended straight line), so dragging/editing the line MOVES the trigger; missing/vertical-in-time points degrade to the endpoint level; deleted drawing → alert lies dormant (no false fires). Crossing semantics = indicator alarms: first check RECORDS the side only (lastSide null-start, no immediate fire), a genuine crossing afterwards fires → status 'triggered' + Telegram message "Trend-line Alert … crossed above/below your trend line (line level X)". Direction is auto-picked as the NEXT crossing (price above line → fires crossing down, and vice-versa). Chart: trend drawings with an active line alert render a 🔔 chip at the line's end point (drawDrawing `alerted` param); the right-click menu replaces the old endpoint-price item with "🔔 Add alert on line" / "🔔 Line alert active — fires when price crosses UP/DOWN" + "Delete line alert"; AlertsDrawer summary "Trend line · crosses up/down · level was $X". Export/import round-trip: drawing ids now ride the export file and are preserved (deduped) on import, so trend alerts re-bind to their drawing; sanitizeAlert carries params.drawingId; ALERT_KINDS += 'trend'. E2E: real UI draw (2 clicks) → menu → "Add alert on line" → API row (kind trend, drawingId, projected 430.26, direction 'above') → chip rendered → deterministic engine proof via real calls: check #1 recorded the side (0 fires), PATCH flipped the drawing slope, check #2 FIRED (target 364.85 = flipped line's projection, message correct, status 'triggered').
- CLONE DRAWING (54.2): "⧉ Clone drawing" in the drawing right-click menu (every kind). cloneDrawing re-projects each point to pixels (xOf/timeAt + yOfPane/valueAtPane — pane-aware), offsets +30px right / +26px down, converts back to (time, price) so the offset is visually consistent at any zoom; falls back to a 2-bar time shift when projection fails; text clones get " (copy)" suffix; color + meta carried over; toast "Trend line cloned / Duplicated slightly below-right". Work Log:
- TOUCH LONG-PRESS = RIGHT CLICK (ChartPanel pointer effect): any touch/pen pointerdown on the chart arms a 550ms hold timer; when it fires (finger still down, <10px drift) the panel opens the SAME ContextMenu as a desktop right-click at the finger position — via a new shared openMenuAt(clientX, clientY, tolMul) that both the native contextmenu handler and the long-press path call (identical hit/price/pane/paneValue semantics: alert here, trend-line alarm, clone, pending/exit menus, trade here, hline…). navigator.vibrate(15) gives haptic feedback where supported. Cancel paths: pointermove drift >10px (pan/zoom), pointerup (quick tap), pointercancel (browser steals the gesture), a second pointerdown (re-arms fresh), Escape still closes an open menu. A drag that started on the same press is abandoned when the menu opens (dragRef/exitDragRef/pendingDragRef nulled, cursor reset) — holding a line opens its menu instead of fighting the drag.
- ANDROID DE-DUPE: Android browsers fire a NATIVE contextmenu ~500ms into a hold (iOS fires nothing) — onContextMenu now records lastNativeCtxAt and cancels the pending timer; fireLongPress skips if a native contextmenu opened within 700ms, so exactly one menu opens on either OS. onContextMenu passes touchDown ? 2 : 1 tolerance.
- FINGER TOLERANCE: hitTest gained a tolMul param (default 1 = byte-identical desktop behavior) scaling every hit tolerance (lines 7→7·tol, points 8→8·tol, position/fib/rect 6→6·tol, ghost chips ±2→±2·tol, text bounds +2·tol pad); touch-originated menus (long-press AND Android native long-press) use tolMul 2 (12-16px) so an imprecise finger still lands on the line it is holding.
- INTERACTIVE EXCLUSION + CSS: pointerdowns on buttons/inputs/[role=button] (chart header, toolbars) don't arm the timer (a held finger there is a slow tap, not a chart menu); menu/ticket pass-through unchanged. Chart container gets [-webkit-touch-callout:none] to suppress iOS callout/selection during holds. ContextMenu clamps now Math.max(4, …) for tiny viewports.
- E2E (agent-browser, 390×844 mobile shell, real login trader+TOTP; synthetic PointerEvent pointerType:'touch' for the holds — Playwright has no touchscreen API — everything else real): watchlist → TSLA → Chart view; long-press @150,300 hit the pending TP line 377.11 → "Change entry/TP/SL…/Cancel pending" menu (tolMul-2 hit proof); Escape closes; long-press @120,520 → empty-chart menu "TSLA · 308.39" → REAL click "Add price alert at 308.39" → API row created (price 308.3939, active) → screenshot t57-lp-menu.png → alert deleted. Work Log:

Probes (scripts-tmp/sr-probe.mjs): synthetic 100/110 oscillator → support ~99.9 (10 touches) + resistance ~110 (19), flat-shelf case detected, minTouches filter works.

Verified against the live login (trader + TOTP via curl): TSLA 1h bars include 08:00 ET pre-market (V≈180), 09:00-15:00 regular and 16:00 ET after-hours buckets — i.e. ext-hours data is ALREADY in the feed and the OPTION had to be built client-side (classify bar OPEN time in America/New_York; crypto is 24/7, 1D+ bars have no session).
- SESSION ENGINE (new src/lib/sessions.ts, client-safe): sessionOf(timeSec) → 'regular' | 'extended' via ONE cached Intl.DateTimeFormat(timeZone:'America/New_York') + Map memo (200k cap) so per-crosshair overlay passes never re-run formatToParts; RTH = 09:30–16:00 ET minutes-of-day (570–960, DST handled by Intl); Intl failure fails OPEN ('regular') so a broken formatter can never blank a chart.
- STORE (src/store/terminal.ts): showExtendedHours (default TRUE = current behavior preserved) + setShowExtendedHours rides the layout blob (LayoutBlob field, DEFAULT_LAYOUT, layoutSnapshot, hydrateFromServer `!== false`); /api/layout stores the blob opaquely so export/import/autosave carry it with zero API changes.
- CHART (ChartPanel.tsx): ① sessionGate = !isCrypto && tf<1D; ethShown = gate && persisted. ② Initial load filters bars to regular session when gate && !eth; scroll-back loadOlder now pages on a RAW cursor (oldestRawTime ≤ displayed oldest) so a chunk of pure pre/post bars can neither stall pagination nor falsely mark history exhausted, then filters the chunk into the display map (zero-added chunks don't shift the visible logical range). ③ applyTick gate: ext-session ticks never roll/mutate RTH bars while ETH off (header quote still moves); quote-poll bar mutation skipped when the last displayed bar isn't regular. ④ renderOverlay draws TradingView-style translucent bands FIRST (behind candles/chrome): per extended bar timeToCoordinate ± half barSpacing, merged neighbor intervals, fill rgba(113,113,122,0.13) over full pane height — only while ethShown. ⑤ Header "ETH" chip (next to price, pointer-events-auto — the ml-auto group is inside the pointer-events-none header) highlighted emerald when on, title "…(Alt+Shift+E)", hidden for crypto/1D+/compact grid panes. ⑥ tt:toggle-eth remote event for the mobile More sheet; __ttDbg.eth() probe {gate, shown, persisted, bars, lastBarSession, extBarCount}.

Task ID: 62
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: "Add a backtest engine for testing and auto tuning the parameters of algo strategies parameters."

Work Log:
- ENGINE (new src/lib/backtest.ts, pure + synchronous): prepareSeries() precomputes ALL param-independent work once per dataset (ET-day ordinals via etDateKey, ET minutes, regular-session flags, prefix sums of close & close² → O(1) SMA/σ per bar); runBacktest() replays one strategy bar-by-bar with O(1)/bar indicators (incremental Wilder RSI for BB, per-bar last-N-changes RSI for RSI-2, session VWAP + opening-range accumulators) and mirrors the LIVE engine exactly: day rollover resets counters/accumulators, regular-session-only accumulator feeding, exits resolved BEFORE entries on the same bar, bb mean-revert / Connors recapture / VWAP-revert exits, rthOnly entry gate, flattenEod (no entries ≥15:45 ET + close at first bar ≥15:45), maxTradesDay, ORB one-per-side memory.
- EXECUTION MODEL (documented in the header): entries fill at NEXT bar open (conservative for close-driven signals), % TP/SL derive from the FILL price and are checked intrabar on every bar INCLUDING the entry bar with STOP-FIRST worst case when both legs fall inside one bar; strategy exits fill at the signal bar's close; no slippage/fees. Open position at end of data is marked-to-market with reason "open at end of data".
- METRICS: trades/W-L/winRate/netPnl+netPct/grossWin/grossLoss/profitFactor (capped 999)/maxDrawdown $+%/avgHold/expectancy/avgWin/avgLoss/largest/longs/shorts/tradesPerWeek/finalEquity/suppressed-gated-signals/openAtEnd + realized equity curve per closed trade.
- OPTIMIZER: grid search over user-ticked numeric param ranges (defs-clamped, ≤64 values/dim, invalid combos skipped: rsiOversold≥rsiOverbought, exitSma≥trendSma), Calmar-style score = netPct / max(maxDDpct, 0.5), minTrades gate scores −∞; compute budget = 12M bar-iterations (≈800 combos max, stride-sampled with a "sampled N/M" badge when the grid is bigger); returns top 100 sorted by score + best + elapsedMs + skipped/truncated counts.
- LIVE-ENGINE PARITY FIXES (algo-engine.ts — the backtest must test the DOCUMENTED strategy, live had drifted): ① rsi2-connors RSI iterated closes[1..rp] — the OLDEST bars of the rolling window — instead of the last rp changes (signal fired on stale data); fixed to iterate i = n−rp..n. ② ORB's firedLong/firedShort were declared + reset daily but NEVER set → "one entry per side per day" was inert; now set on successful entry placement. ③ ET helpers (etDateKey/etMinutesOfDay) deduplicated into sessions.ts (engine now imports them; backtest shares the same source of truth).
- API (new POST /api/algo/backtest, requireUser): validates strategy/tf(1m|5m|15m)/symbol/qty/capital; fetches real history via getBars({lookbackDays}) (same source the live engine polls; Alpaca IEX for stocks, paginated Binance for crypto), drops the forming bar, caps 1m≤120d / 5m≤365d / 15m≤730d and 120k bars; mode:'run' → runBacktest, mode:'optimize' → optimizeBacktest(ranges, minTrades); stateless (no DB rows — results live in the client).
- UI: new BacktestPanel (tabs/BacktestPanel.tsx) + AlgoTab header segmented control "Live runs | Backtest" — the backtest shares the run-setup config bundle (symbol/tf/strategy/params/qty/TP-SL/cap/RTH/EOD) so what you tune is exactly what you deploy. Panel: history-days + starting-capital inputs, fill-model disclosure, Run backtest → 8 metric cards (color-coded) + recharts realized-equity AreaChart (downsampled ≤700 pts) + trades table (newest-first, ≤200 rows, scrollable) + "no trades fired" hint; Auto-tune ▸ per-param tick + min/max/step prefilled from the strategy defs, min-trades input, Run optimizer → top-25 Calmar-ranked table (params summary/score/net%/PF/WR/DD%/trades, "best" badge, sampled/skipped/elapsed badges), row click OR "Apply best to run setup" copies the combo into the setup params (merged + normalized, params section auto-opens) with a toast.
- MOBILE GAP FIXED: MobileShell (Task 56) predates the algo tab — mobile had NO path to algo trading at all. Added 'algo' to the tool views (menu row "Algo Trading" with Rocket icon → full-screen AlgoTab inside the tool chrome with proper title + back button).

- E2E — engine (synthetic bars, bun scripts-tmp/backtest-smoke.ts + -smoke2.ts): 4 strategies produce trades + sane ledgers; maxTradesDay=1 → ≤1 entry/day; ORB onePerSide → ≤2/day; EOD flatten + strategy exits + band-mean/VWAP-revert reasons fire; SL-first worst case verified with a bar spanning both legs (books the stop); equity curve never exceeds its peak and reconciles with finalEquity; optimizer sampling/sorting/invalid-skip correct; PERF: 300 combos × 40k bars = 731ms wall (budget cap works).

Task ID: 63
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: "no matter what number i changed in Backtest - History (calendar days) 360 -> 730. The earliest trade date is Jul 16 2026. pls check" — diagnose why changing the backtest history window had no effect on the earliest tradeable date, and fix.

Work Log:
- Diagnosis via dev.log + live probes (scripts-tmp/probe-history.mjs, probe-alpaca-crypto.mjs): three distinct data-floor defects found.
  (1) STOCKS: fetchBarsPaged (alpaca.ts) had maxPages=15; IEX pages are only ~2k bars, so a 730d 5m request truncated at ~31k bars and — because pages walk OLDEST→newest — silently DROPPED THE NEWEST 5 MONTHS (result ended 2026-05-08). 360d happened to fit, so 360 vs 730 produced wildly different (and wrong) windows.
  (2) CRYPTO (the user's exact report): Binance klines are hard-capped at 10k bars (12-page backward walk) → BTC/USD 15m always returned exactly 9,991 bars starting 2026-07-09 whether days=360 or 730 → first trade Jul 16 2026, completely independent of the setting.
  (3) SILENT CAPS: MAX_DAYS per timeframe (1m 120 / 5m 365 / 15m 730) clamped the request invisibly, and the results header claimed "last N days" based on the request, never the actual data.
- Fixes in src/lib/alpaca.ts: fetchBarsPaged default maxPages 15→60 (+ truncation now logs a warning); new GetBarsOptions.maxPages; new getAlpacaCryptoBarsDeep() — deep crypto lookbacks (>9k bars) fetch via Alpaca v1beta3 BACKWARD in time slices anchored at `now` (slices ≤180d ≈ 28k bars ≤ 40 pages, 40-segment cap, 45s wall-clock budget, dedupe) so the NEWEST bars are always complete even when the window can't be fully covered; Binance stays first for chart-width windows and as fallback. Removed a duplicate `tf` declaration introduced by the restructure.
- Fixes in src/app/api/algo/backtest/route.ts: clamp uses shared ALGO_MAX_DAYS (exported from algo-strategies.ts, also imported by the UI); meta now carries from/to (actual bar window), coveredDays, source (binance/alpaca), dataCapped flag; new 2-min TTL bars cache (per user|symbol|tf|days, ≤12 entries) so the tune→re-run→optimize loop doesn't re-pay the 6–35s deep fetch on every request.
- Fixes in src/components/terminal/tabs/BacktestPanel.tsx: label shows "max {N} for {tf}"; days input visibly clamps on blur; crypto symbols get a feed-cap hint line; results header shows the ACTUAL data window with year ("data Oct 21, 24 → Oct 21, 26 (730d) · feed: alpaca") and an amber role="note" when coverage < requested ("Requested 120d, but the data feed only returned 88d … replayed Jul 25, 26 → Oct 21, 26 instead of the full window").
- Probe verification (probe-history2.mjs): TSLA 5m 730d → 40,822 bars 2024-10-21→2026-10-21 (was truncated at 2026-05-08); BTC/USD 15m 730d → 86,252 bars 2024-05→now (was 9,991 from Jul 9); BTC/USD 5m 360d → 111,616 bars (was 9,991 from Sep 16); all monotonic, all ending at "now".
- E2E (agent-browser, real login/TOTP): 5m 730 visibly clamps to 365 + hint; TSLA 15m 730d → "data Oct 21, 24 → Oct 21, 26 (730d) · 14,316 bars · trades Oct 21, 24 → Oct 21, 26"; BTC/USD 15m 730d → "data May 4, 24 → Oct 21, 26 (900d) · 86,251 bars · feed: alpaca" with the crypto hint visible; optimizer re-validated on 86k bars (4 combos, 0.1s replay, BEST row + apply intact); BTC/USD 1m 120d exercised the deadline guard + amber notice (88d covered, 120k newest bars); cached re-run completes in 239ms (was 36s). Task ID: 64
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: "There is a issue found when VWAP algo submit order. the SL value is not appear in the position. while the setup SL is has a value of 1%."

Work Log:
- DB forensics: AlgoRun/AlgoLog tables empty (user deleted the run, which wipes logs) — traced the code path instead: AlgoTab.tsx sends slMode/slValue correctly -> /api/algo persists them -> algo-engine computes SL from the live quote and calls placeOrderWithExits.
- LIVE repro (market open, paper keys from DB): STOCK market buy 1xF with SL-only 1% -> Alpaca 422 "bracket orders require take_profit.limit_price" — the WHOLE entry is rejected, so an algo run configured with TP=none + SL=1% can never place its stop (and a manual one-sided ticket fails identically). Alpaca's `bracket` class requires BOTH legs.
- Verified alternatives live: order_class='oto' with SL-only -> 200, fills, stop leg activates on the position (SL 11.79 visible as an open stop). Crypto path (BTC/USD SL-only via placeOrderWithExits + ttb plan) verified healthy end-to-end: entry filled 14:19:26, stop_limit SL leg attached 14:19:27, stayed until cleanup closed the position.
- FIX 1 (src/lib/trading.ts placeOrderWithExits): stock path now uses order_class = both legs ? 'bracket' : 'oto' — one-sided TP/SL plans no longer 422.
- FIX 2 (src/lib/trading.ts OCO supervisor leg-heal): filled 'oto' parents now get the same naked-position leg-heal as brackets.
- FIX 3 (src/lib/trading.ts replacePendingEntrySmart buildPlaceBody): cancel+replace rebuild mirrors the rule (one-sided -> 'oto') so dragging the SL/TP on a queued one-sided entry can't die on the 422 and lose the exits.
- FIX 4 (src/lib/algo-engine.ts): run log no longer swallows r.errors/splitNote after a successful entry — "Exit-leg warning: ..." / split-attach notes are journaled (previously a failed crypto OCO attach was invisible, making "SL configured but missing" undiagnosable).
- LIVE verification of the fixed code: placeOrderWithExits SL-only -> filled, SL leg active @11.81; TP+SL regression -> native bracket, limit+stop legs both active; queued GTC OTO + SL drag via replacePendingEntrySmart -> cancel-replace landed as oto with the new stop (10.98 -> 10.75). All test orders/positions cleaned up (cancels + closePositionFull; final account snapshot shows only the user's own pre-existing NVDA/ECHO/MCD/BTC orders).
- bun run lint clean; dev.log no compile errors; /api/trading/positions + /api/algo smoke 401 (auth gate OK, module chain healthy).

Stage Summary:
- Root cause: Alpaca rejects one-sided brackets ("bracket orders require take_profit.limit_price") — any STOCK algo run (VWAP/BB/ORB/Connors) or manual ticket configured with only SL (or only TP) had its entry rejected, so the SL never reached the position. Crypto was never affected (emulated OCO attach verified working).
- One-sided exit plans now place as order_class 'oto' at all three sites (initial entry, naked-position leg-heal eligibility, drag cancel+replace); algo run log surfaces exit-leg failures.
- Header doc of trading.ts updated with the verified bracket/oto API contract.

Work Log:
- BUG 1 (ETH = Ethereum symbol — chart blank): reproduced live — opening ETH/USD left the chart spinning forever. Root cause chain: ① Binance was IP-banned (HTTP 418 on BOTH bases, seen in dev.log) so getBinanceBars() returned []; ② the Alpaca fall-through hit a unit bug — cryptoLookback = tf.barCount (900 = a BAR count) used as DAYS → needCryptoBars = 259,200 > 9000 → the 45s-budget getAlpacaCryptoBarsDeep() deep-fetch fired for a mere 900-bar chart window → /api/market/bars took 47s (dev.log: "200 in 47s") → spinner + no chart. (The same unit bug also inflated the direct Alpaca crypto lookback.)
- FIX 1a (src/lib/alpaca.ts getBars): cryptoLookbackDays now derived from barCount × tf.seconds (900×300s = ~5 days for 5m) in BOTH the deep-path guard and the Alpaca crypto branch — deep-fetch now triggers only for genuine >9k-bar lookbacks (backtest/S-R with explicit days); ordinary crypto charts fetch ~1 page.
- FIX 1b (src/lib/binance.ts): 418/429 circuit breaker — after a ban signal all Binance calls no-op for 5 minutes (quotes fall back to Alpaca snapshots, bars to Alpaca) instead of hammering and extending the IP ban every 15s.
- VERIFY: ETH/USD 5m bars 47s → 0.17s (200, source binance after ban lifted); browser mobile + desktop: ETH/USD chart renders candles/volume/MAs/live quote; console "ETH/USD · live Binance data". Note: the ETH trading-hours TOGGLE was also re-tested OFF→ON on desktop + mobile (TSLA 5m: 170↔187 bars) — it was never broken; "ETH" in the report meant the symbol.
- FEATURE 2 (order source attribution): new client_order_id grammar — manual/plan entries keep `ttb-<m>-tp<enc>-sl<enc>-<rand6>`; ALGO entries now encode `ttr-<m>-<runRef8>-tp<enc>-sl<enc>-<rand4>` (48-char-safe with a graceful fallback to ttb- on overflow). algo-engine passes sourceRef = first 8 of the AlgoRun id; encodePlanClientId/parseCryptoPlan (trading.ts, exported) extended with the runRef; the drag cancel+replace rebuild preserves the original's runRef so edits never de-attribute a run.
- Trade history: FIFO matcher carries the entry lot's clientOrderId → ClosedTrade gains source:'manual'|'algo' + runRef (src/lib/trade-history.ts); /api/trading/history batch-resolves runRefs → strategy display names via AlgoRun startsWith query; PendingOrderRow (positions route) gains source + strategy the same way (dedup'd the plan decoder into the exported parseCryptoPlan).
- UI: Positions tab Pending orders + Trade history tables got a sortable SOURCE column — zinc "Manual" vs violet "Algo · <Strategy>" badges (SourceBadge).
- FEATURE 3 (Report): /api/trading/history consumers get the attribution; new "Manual vs Algo — profit/loss" section (recharts grouped bars: gross profit WIN_C vs gross loss LOSS_C per source; learned that per-entry data `fill` creates implicit recharts Cells that override Bar fill — removed) + "Trade source breakdown" panel (Manual N trades W/L net · Algo N trades W/L net · per-strategy rows) + counts line under the chart; empty-algo hint when no algo trades yet.

- RESTORE/cleanup: test run deleted (runs:0), position flat, stop legs canceled, Report tab closed via confirm, first chart tab symbol/timeframe restored to TSLA 1D (515 bars), viewport restored, browser closed. Console zero page errors. Lint clean; tsc 0 src errors. dev.log flagged one transient hot-reload ReferenceError (binanceBannedUntil referenced between the two edits) — resolved, 0 recurrences.
- Files: src/lib/alpaca.ts (unit fix), src/lib/binance.ts (breaker), src/lib/trading.ts (ttr grammar + sourceRef), src/lib/algo-engine.ts (sourceRef), src/lib/trade-history.ts (source decode), src/lib/market-types.ts (ClosedTrade.source/runRef/strategy), src/app/api/trading/history/route.ts (+strategy resolve), src/app/api/trading/positions/route.ts (pending source/strategy, dedup parser), src/components/chart/trading-types.ts (PendingOrderInfo.source), src/components/terminal/tabs/PositionsTab.tsx (SourceBadge + 2 columns), src/components/terminal/tabs/ReportTab.tsx (Manual vs Algo section). Probes: scripts-tmp/t65-*.mjs.

Stage Summary:
- Crypto charts can no longer hang for ~47s when Binance bans the sandbox IP: the days-vs-bars unit bug that routed every ordinary chart through the 45s deep fetch is fixed at both call sites, and a 5-minute 418/429 circuit breaker stops the ban from being fed. ETH/USD (and every crypto symbol) now loads in well under a second either from Binance or the corrected Alpaca fall-through.
