Task ID: 67
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: "Fix: 1. right click in chart to update the percentage of a trailing does not work."

Work Log:
- FORENSICS (scripts-tmp/t67-inspect-trails.mjs): the user's real trailing stops (NVDA sell trail 2%, ECHO sell trail 3%) carry stop_price + trail_percent, so the chart DOES draw/hit the "SL trail X%" line — the failure was downstream. Lib probe (t67-patch-trail.mjs) reproduced it: PATCH /v2/orders/{id} with {trail_percent:"2.5"} on a trail-2% order → Alpaca 422 "order parameters are not changed" → replaceExitOrderSmart's shortcut trusted the message and returned success ("Order already at this value") → the client toasted "Trail % updated" while the order stayed at 2%. Body-shape matrix (t67-patch-forms/2.mjs) proved Alpaca simply IGNORES trail_percent on trailing_stop PATCHes (trail-only, +type/tif, +qty, string/number forms all 422 "not changed"; adding stop_price → "trailing_stop orders must not have stop_price") — an in-place trail change is impossible on Alpaca; cancel+re-place is the only way.
- FIX (src/lib/trading.ts): new patchAlreadyApplied(cur, patch) — verifies the order's CURRENT trail_percent/stop_price/limit_price against every value the patch asks for, at the precision the PATCH body was formatted with (fmtPx granularity: stocks 2dp/4dp, crypto 6dp/8dp). Both 422-"not changed" shortcuts (main host loop + replaced-successor retry) now only short-circuit when the values REALLY match; otherwise the request falls through to the existing cancel+re-place fallback, which re-places the trailing stop with the new trail via POST (exitBodyFrom already preserves type=trailing_stop + new trail_percent). Same-value drags keep the zero-churn shortcut (regression-safe); stop/limit edits are unaffected (their 422s genuinely mean "already there").
- VERIFIED — lib: 2→2.5 cancel+replace landed (new id, trail "2.5"), 2.5→2 restored, repeat patch at 2 → "already at this value" with UNCHANGED order id (no churn).
- VERIFIED — browser (real login, the user's live NVDA trailing stop): overlay-canvas pixel-scan located the rose SL line; synthetic contextmenu on it → exit menu ("Stop loss · trail 2%" / ✏️ Change trail %… / Cancel SL order) → editor → 2.5 → Apply → /api/trading/positions shows trailPercent 2.5 with a NEW order id and the chart chip re-rendered "SL trail 2.5%" at the fresh ratcheted stop; dblclick on the line opens the same "Set trail % (stop distance from price)" editor (shared code path); restored to 2% through the same UI and re-verified. ZERO page errors.
- E2E note: clicking the wrong canvas (lightweight-charts pane vs the ChartPanel overlay canvas where the SL/TP/pending lines live) opens the generic chart menu — the hit-test reads container coords and the overlay canvas fills the container exactly, so real user clicks on the VISIBLE line always land on the overlay; only the synthetic probe needed the pixel scan.
- RESTORE: NVDA trail back at 2% (new Alpaca id — Alpaca recomputes the ratcheted stop on every re-place; the % matches the user's original), chart symbol switched back to TSLA 1D, layout tabs intact, viewport 1280×800, browser closed. Lint clean; tsc 0 src errors; dev.log clean.
- Files: src/lib/trading.ts (patchAlreadyApplied + 2 guarded shortcuts). Probes: scripts-tmp/t67-inspect-trails.mjs, t67-patch-trail.mjs, t67-patch-forms.mjs, t67-patch-forms2.mjs, t67-patch-same.mjs.

Stage Summary:
- Right-clicking a trailing-stop line on the chart and changing the trail % now actually works: Alpaca cannot PATCH a trailing_stop's trail_percent in place (it 422s "order parameters are not changed" while silently ignoring the field — the exact message it also uses for genuine no-ops, which the old shortcut trusted blindly), so the terminal now verifies the current order values before honoring that shortcut and otherwise routes the change through the cancel+re-place fallback, re-placing the trailing stop with the new percent. Same-value edits still short-circuit without churn; verified end-to-end against the user's real NVDA trailing stop (2% → 2.5% → 2%) from the chart's right-click menu.

Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: "Fix: 1. ETH - extended trading Hour. Bar in chart is not found. 2. The issue exist. Order from Algo has TP and SL. but again the SL order is not found in the position once filled."

Work Log:
- BUG 2 forensics (user's screenshot: TSLA ORB run, log "ENTRY BUY 10 TSLA · TP 391.88 · SL 382.23" but Positions shows "+ SL"): the AlgoRun table was empty (run deleted), so the live paper account was probed directly (t68-alpaca-orders.mjs). The algo bracket family is REAL and healthy on the broker — parent market buy e97340d2 filled, TP leg limit sell 391.88 status=new, SL leg stop sell 382.23 status=**held** (Alpaca OCO: exactly one leg works, the sibling is armed/held). Two compounding defects hid it: (a) getOpenOrders only fetches status=open and Alpaca EXCLUDES 'held' from that filter (verified: flat open list had 0 held rows; held legs only appear NESTED under OPEN parents — COIN/META oco, MCD pending bracket) — a FILLED parent is closed, so its armed leg is invisible to a status=open query entirely; (b) the positions route's held-leg rule (`o.status==='held' && (!parent || parent.side!==o.side)`) skipped flat held legs anyway. Result: TP visible, SL column empty ("+ SL") — exactly the report. NVDA/ECHO manual stops were unaffected (status=new), which is why only algo/manual bracket SLs vanished.
- FIX 2a (src/lib/trading.ts getOpenOrders): new optional heldSymbols param — a third parallel fetch `status=all&limit=500&direction=desc&symbols=<position symbols>` (symbols-scoped → small payload; position symbols passed in ORDER-symbol form — crypto keeps the slash) whose rows are filtered to WORKING_ORDER_STATUSES (new/accepted/pending_new/accepted_for_bidding/pending_replace/held/done_for_day) and merged by id (nested still wins when it carries legs). All three fetches run in Promise.all.
- FIX 2b (src/app/api/trading/positions/route.ts): positions are fetched FIRST, heldSymbols = the position symbols drive the sweep (when a chart symbol is requested the sweep narrows to that symbol); new pendingFamilyLegIds set (ids of legs nested under NOT-filled parents) and a rewritten visit() rule — a flat (parentless) held leg is a real exit UNLESS it also nests under an open unfilled family (pending entry's future leg); a held leg WITH a parent on the opposite side is skipped only when the parent is NOT filled (the crypto-OCO same-side armed sibling and filled-parent cases still map, preserving the Task 64-era fixes).
- VERIFY 2: live route now returns exits.TSLA.sl stop@382.23 + tp@391.88 — AND recovered two MORE positions that were silently missing their armed SLs (SPCX stop@161.75, MSTR stop@151.91); COIN/META oco held legs, NVDA/ECHO trailing stops, the short's buy-side legs and the pending MCD bracket (tp/sl in the PENDING list, NOT as exits — no phantom) all correct; Positions tab renders SL 382.23 on the TSLA row and the chart draws the "SL 382.23" exit line (desktop + mobile 390×844). Fresh-bracket lifecycle probe (t68-fresh-bracket.mjs): placeOrderWithExits market buy 1xF with TP+SL → filled @11.93 → held SL leg stop@11.81 recovered by getOpenOrders → maps to exits.F.sl → closePositionFull cleanup (canceled 1 exit; account back to exactly the user's 7 pre-test positions).
- BUG 1 forensics ("ETH — extended trading hours, bar not found"): reproduced with a real login — toggling the chart's ETH button throws an uncaught lightweight-charts/fancy-canvas **"Object is disposed"** Runtime Error on EVERY toggle (Next dev overlay pops and covers the screen; the chart is mid-recreation so it looks broken/no bars — the report's "bar not found"). Root cause: the chart-creation effect lists showExtendedHours in its deps, so the ETH toggle disposes and recreates the whole chart; lw-charts 4.2.3's destroy() only cancels the paint rAF planned BEFORE remove(), but the ChartModel captured a `.bind()` copy of the scheduler at construction (line 11047) — any resize-driven invalidation that lands during/after the DOM teardown re-arms the rAF paint loop, and that late paint reads the DISPOSED fancy-canvas binding and throws (full stack captured live: DevicePixelContentBoxBinding.get ← tryCreateCanvasRenderingTarget2D ← TimeAxisWidget._internal_paint ← rAF callback). A minimal repro page with the vendored build did NOT throw (needs the app's resize churn), so the fix was validated against the real app.
- FIX 1 (src/components/chart/ChartPanel.tsx chart-effect cleanup): immediately before chart.remove(), neuter the dying instance's paint scheduler — replace BOTH `_private__chartWidget._private__invalidateHandler` AND `_private__chartWidget._private__model._private__invalidateHandler` (the model's bound copy is the one that actually schedules; patching the widget method alone was proven insufficient live) with no-ops. Anything already queued is canceled inside remove(). Property names pinned by the exact "lightweight-charts": "4.2.3" dependency (no caret).
- VERIFY 1: after the fix, 6 then 8 rapid ETH toggles (700ms apart) → ZERO window errors (before: 4-8 errors per toggle); desktop + mobile charts render correctly in both ETH states (RTH-only vs pre/post-market bars), crosshair OHLC legend updates (5m premarket bar values), TP/SL/position lines and SMA/EMA overlays intact, no Next issue badge; chart tf + ETH state restored to the user's original (1D / ETH on); viewport 1280×800; browser closed. Lint clean; tsc 0 src errors; dev.log: no 429s, no new errors (the single old binanceBannedUntil HMR transient at line 4046 predates this session — Task 65's noted leftover).
- Files: src/lib/trading.ts (getOpenOrders held-leg sweep + WORKING_ORDER_STATUSES), src/app/api/trading/positions/route.ts (sequential positions→orders, sweep scoping, pendingFamilyLegIds + visit() rewrite), src/components/chart/ChartPanel.tsx (dying-chart scheduler neuter in cleanup). Probes: scripts-tmp/t68-alpaca-orders.mjs, t68-open-filter.mjs, t68-verify-edge.mjs, t68-fresh-bracket.mjs, t68-check-run.mjs (superseded).

Stage Summary:
- A filled stock bracket's armed stop-loss leg is no longer invisible: getOpenOrders now sweeps a symbols-scoped status=all window for the working orders of symbols that have positions (held legs only exist there), and the positions route maps flat held legs onto the position as real exits while still hiding pending-entry families' future legs. The user's TSLA ORB position now shows TP 391.88 + SL 382.23 in the Positions tab and draws the SL line on the chart; SPCX and MSTR turned out to have the same hidden-SL defect and were healed by the same fix. Verified from three angles: the user's live bracket, the full live-account matrix (crypto OCO / shorts / trails / pending brackets), and a fresh algo-path bracket lifecycle placed and cleaned up on the paper account.
- Toggling Extended Trading Hours no longer crashes the chart: the lw-charts 4.2.3 teardown race (post-destroy paint via the model's bound scheduler) is neutralized at cleanup, so the "Object is disposed" Runtime Error overlay is gone and both ETH states render correct bars. The same patch protects every chart recreation path (symbol/timeframe/chart-type/indicator-settings changes share the effect).

Gate order: session → EOD window → cooldown (silent) → fresh-signal → daily cap. startAlgoRun persists both + mentions them in the start log.

Task ID: 72
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: "Fix: Algo Trading->VWAP->Strategy Parameter->Deviation% is limited to integer. This should accept decimal like 0.6."

Work Log:
- ROOT CAUSE: not a clamping bug — `normalizeAlgoParams` only min/max-clamps (devPct 0.2..5 passes 0.6 through untouched) and the engine/backtest use devPct in raw float compares with default 0.6. The integer limitation was a classic controlled-input decimal swallow: the strategy-parameter Inputs in AlgoTab.tsx + AlgoRunSettings.tsx used `value={String(params[key])}` + `onChange → Number(text)`; typing "0." parses to 0, state re-renders "0" and DELETES the dot, so a decimal could never be typed ("0.6" ended up "06" → 6). All other algo numeric fields (qty/TP%/SL%/cooldown/maxTrades, backtest range NumIn) already hold strings and were unaffected.
- FIX (both files, same shape): per-key draft-text overlay — new `paramDraft: Record<string,string>` state; input value = `paramDraft[key] ?? String(params[key] ?? '')`; onChange stores the raw text AND parses into numeric params when finite (save path always current); onBlur drops the draft so the display snaps to the canonical `String(number)` (".8" → "0.8", "0." → "0"); drafts cleared whenever params are re-seeded (strategy pick, loadConfigIntoForm, settings-dialog open effect).
- E2E (real login trader+TOTP, 1440×900): setup form — clicked into VWAP Fade Deviation %, typed "0.6" with per-char keystrokes → "0.6" survives; blur → "0.6" (numeric state proven); ".8" → blur "0.8"; "1.25" → "1.25"; instrumented window.fetch + mock-routed /api/algo (NO run created) → Start button POST body captured: `"strategy":"vwap-fade","params":{"devPct":0.6,...}`. Settings dialog — seeded a SAFE stopped ZZTEST run via db (fake symbol → engine can never trade), opened ⚙ editor, typed 0.6 → Save → DB row reads `params:{devPct:0.6,allowShort:1}`. (During the mock phase the GET /api/algo poll briefly swallowed `{"error":"mock"}` and latched the Next error boundary — reload recovered it; test artifact, not a code bug.) RESTORE: ZZTEST run + its log deleted (algoRun 0 / algoLog 0), Algo Trading tab removed via confirm (JS click per known Radix pattern) → tab set [TSLA, Heat Map, Economic Calendar, Positions], zero page errors, browser closed. Lint clean; dev.log no compile errors.
- Files: src/components/terminal/tabs/AlgoTab.tsx (draft-overlay param input + 2 draft-clear sites), src/components/terminal/tabs/AlgoRunSettings.tsx (same). Probe: scripts-tmp/t72-seed.mjs. Screenshot: screens-tmp/t72-restored.png.

Stage Summary:
- Deviation % (and every numeric strategy parameter in the algo setup form AND the per-run ⚙ settings dialog) now accepts decimals exactly as typed — 0.6, .8, 1.25 all survive keystroke-by-keystroke entry, commit to numeric state on blur, flow through POST/PATCH bodies, and persist to the DB; engine + backtest consumed decimal devPct all along, so no backend change was needed.

  • BACKTEST (backtest.ts + route): BacktestConfig gains OPTIONAL riskBasis/riskMoneyPct (pre-78e probe configs still compile); 'pct' resolves each trade's budget from the CURRENT SIMULATED EQUITY (cash) — risk compounds with the curve exactly like a live account; route validates pct ∈ (0,100] for risk+pct, riskMoney > 0 for risk+usd.

Task: "Enhence: 1) algo export/import knows enable/disable status 2) config export/import knows Strategy Orders enable/disable 3) warning if same symbol has two enabled algo strategies 4) Positions tab wrongly tags some algo symbols as Manual"

Work Log:
- ALGO EXPORT/IMPORT CARRIES RUN STATE (79-1): AlgoConfigFileEntry gains optional `enabled` (export-time run state; envelope stays v2 — purely additive, legacy files import as running exactly as before). parseAlgoConfigImport reads it from the {name, config, enabled} wrapper (probe-verified round-trip: enabled preserved, legacy/bare/array forms default undefined). Engine: startAlgoRun(cfg, { autostart }) — autostart:false creates the row status 'stopped' with a "Run created STOPPED (imported config) — press Start" journal and NO engine. POST /api/algo accepts autoStart (default true); live-mode refusal now only applies to STARTING (a stopped run places no orders, so stopped-run creation is safe even in live). Exports (per-run + export-all) stamp `enabled: r.status === 'running'`; import dialog shows "was RUNNING"/"was STOPPED" badges, POSTs autoStart per entry, splits the toast ("Started 1 · Imported 1 stopped") and relabels the footer button ("Import 2 (start 1)" / "Import N stopped").
- USER CONFIG EXPORT/IMPORT KEEPS STRATEGY ARM STATE (79-2): /api/user/config GET now exports strategies[].status; sanitizeStrategy maps armed→armed, paused→paused, placing/triggered/failed→paused (re-arming a fired one-shot would surprise-fire it again; legacy files without status default to armed = previous behavior). Import summary gains strategiesArmed/strategiesPaused; SettingsDialog confirm shows "N strategy rule(s) (X armed, Y paused — arm state preserved)". E2E round-trip: paused rule exported "SPY:paused" → imported with strategiesArmed 0/strategiesPaused 1 → rule still paused.
- DUPLICATE-SYMBOL WARNING (79-3): starting a run (setup form) or restarting a stopped run while ANOTHER run on the SAME symbol is 'running' opens an AlertDialog — lists the live run(s) by strategy·symbol·timeframe, explains double exposure, offers Cancel / "Start anyway" (deliberate override; force flag skips the re-check). Import dialog gained the passive sibling: amber banner when a selected config targets a symbol with an already-enabled run. AlgoTab computes runningSymbols/dupRunning from the polled runs list.
- POSITIONS SOURCE FIX (79-4): TWO root causes — (a) server: openLots (FIFO) are keyed by ORDER symbols ('BTC/USD' keeps the slash) while POSITION symbols drop it ('BTCUSD'), so openLots.get(p.symbol) missed EVERY crypto position → source undefined; (b) client: PositionsTab SourceBadge rendered "Manual" for BOTH source='manual' AND source=undefined (unknown). With "Regular hours only" OFF the session gate (rthOnly) does not suppress entries. BUT three equity-market leaks still broke true 7x24: (1) "Flatten before close" (flattenEod, DEFAULT ON) closed any open position at 15:45 ET daily and blocked entries 15:45→midnight ET; (2) stored/imported rows with the flags true were still honored by the engine for crypto; (3) feedAccumulators only consumed REGULAR-session bars (09:30–16:00 ET) — vwap-fade's session VWAP starved overnight (vwapDen=0 → no signals) and orb15 had no overnight opening range, so those two strategies could NEVER fire outside RTH even with everything off. The backtester (parity contract) had the same three behaviors.
- ENGINE (src/lib/algo-engine.ts): crypto symbols (contain '/') now IGNORE rthOnly/flattenEod whatever the stored flags say — `rthGateActive`/`eodActive` both ANDed with !isCrypto — with a one-time journal line "Crypto symbol — equity session guards (RTH-only / EOD flatten) ignored — this run trades 24/7" (st.cryptoNoted, never-silent pattern). feedAccumulators gained an isCrypto param: crypto feeds on ALL bars of the ET day (session VWAP = running VWAP since ET midnight; orb15's OR window anchored at ET midnight instead of 09:30), so the day-accumulator strategies genuinely evaluate 24/7. Header comment updated.
- BACKTEST (src/lib/backtest.ts, parity): same semantics — EOD flatten + RTH gate skip for cfg.isCrypto (the field already existed for sizing increments), accumulator feed `rth[i]===1 || cfg.isCrypto`, OR anchor 0 for crypto; header contract updated.
- UI: AlgoTab + AlgoRunSettings — when the symbol is crypto both session-guard switches render forced-off + DISABLED with hints ("crypto trades 24/7 — session guard not applicable" / "…no daily close to flatten at"); save bodies send rthOnly/flattenEod=false for crypto; symbol labels now "Symbol — stock or crypto" (placeholder TSLA · BTC/USD); run cards show a "24/7" badge for crypto and hide the RTH/EOD badges there.

Task ID: 82c
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: "Please check level 1 = s/r anchor, is this a correct usage of FIB retracement? I am in doubt."

Work Log:
- ANSWER (convention): yes — in a retracement 1.000 sits at the leg ORIGIN and 0.000 at the leg END (the extreme); 0.5–0.618 is measured back from the extreme toward the origin. TradingView labels a fib drawn origin→extreme exactly this way, so "1 = the S/R level, 0 = the swing extreme" is the standard orientation — PROVIDED the level really is where the move started.
- DOUBT VALIDATED (the real defect): the old code anchored 0.000 on the whole-LOOKBACK extreme (365d high/low), which may belong to a completely different move than the S/R level — a synthetic leg whose golden pocket has no classic meaning (e.g. TSLA showed swing 297.43 vs anchor 383.77 regardless of whether price ever traveled that leg).
- FIX (real-leg construction, src/components/chart/TradeAdvisor.tsx): new touchEpisodes() (bars within tolPct of the level, gaps ≤3 bars merged = one test) + legFromLevel() — scans episodes newest-first, takes the most recent REAL leg: departure after the episode → subsequent extreme, bounded by the level's next revisit (completed leg) or now (ongoing leg); requires ≥3 bars away and the extreme clearing the zone by 2×tol on the trade side. analyze() now walks candidates strongest-first and uses the first level WITH a real leg; auto-side prefers a real leg before score/proximity. Only if NO candidate has one does it fall back to the whole-lookback extreme — now flagged: Advice.legSource 'leg'|'lookback', panel "Fib leg" row (dates, tooltip) + ⚠ synthetic warning paragraph, toast shows the leg window.
- DRAWING: the advisor fib's two points now sit at their REAL bars — (departure bar, level) → (swing bar, extreme) — so the drawn fib is a true diagonal like a hand-drawn TradingView fib (ChartPanel's Task 82b leg rendering shows it); fallback still anchors both ends at the swing bar. meta gains legSource.
- LOGIC PROBE (bun -e, synthetic series): ongoing leg wins by recency over an older bigger leg; flat/no-departure → null (fallback); short side mirrors correctly.
- E2E (agent-browser, real trader+TOTP, TSLA 1D): plan now SHORT on a REAL leg — anchor 383.77 resistance score 6, leg Oct 12 → Oct 16, swing 349.41 (not the lookback 297.43), pocket 366.59–370.65 = exactly 0.5/0.618 of the 34.36 leg, entry 368.62 (0.559), SL 385.80 (anchor + half pocket), TP 349.41 (the actual leg low), R:R 1.12; chart shows the diagonal leg tracing the real decline with all 7 level labels exact; monitor "price overshot the zone" + marketable warning correct at 374.70 (73.6% retrace). Zero console/page errors; lint clean; tsc clean in src.
- Files: src/components/chart/TradeAdvisor.tsx. Screenshot: t82c-fib-realleg.png.

Stage Summary:
- The advisor fib is now a textbook retracement: 1.000 = the S/R level the move actually departed from, 0.000 = that leg's extreme, golden pocket = 0.5–0.618 back from the extreme, drawn as a real diagonal between the two real bars. When no real leg exists the panel says so (⚠ synthetic) instead of silently drawing a meaningless pocket.

Task ID: 84
Agent: main
Task: Investigate live-vs-backtest discrepancy ("the result and the actual is different") and fix parity gaps.

Work Log:
- Read both engines end-to-end: src/lib/backtest.ts (replay) vs src/lib/algo-engine.ts (live 15s cycle) + data paths (backtest route, bars-cache, alpaca getBars, binance) + trading.ts fill path.
- Exported evalStrategy from algo-engine.ts (pure, unchanged behavior) so probes can diff live decisions against backtest decisions.
- Built scripts-tmp/parity-probe.ts — replays the SAME real bars (BTC/USD 5m via Binance, no keys) through runBacktest (gates off) AND a faithful live-cycle sim (evalStrategy + accumulators, exits-before-entries), diffing accepted-entry sequences per strategy.
- First run surfaced 3 apparent mismatches; debugging (scripts-tmp/parity-debug2.ts) proved 2 were probe artifacts (my sim forgot the per-day VWAP/OR accumulator reset; orb marked-trade filter). ONE genuine engine divergence remained: warmup off-by-one — live guard `n < period+2` allows the first decision at i=period+1, backtest warmup `+2` started at i=period+2, so a live run could fire ONE BAR before the backtest on identical data.
- Fixed backtest.ts warmup: bb-reversion and rsi2-connors now `+1` (aligned bar-for-bar to evalStrategy guards); header PARITY CONTRACT updated.
- Quantified execution divergence on the same data (rsi2, 52 trades): fill-timing alone ≈ 0% delta; +2bps slippage → −17.3pp; +2bps slip +10bps fees → −103.8pp vs backtest. THE discrepancy is costs + live friction, not signals.
- Added Task 84 cost model to backtest.ts: slippageBps (adverse drift on market/stop fills: entries, SL legs, strategy/EOD/mark exits — TP resting limits fill clean) + feeBps (per-fill notional fee, entry+exit). Both optional, default 0 = byte-identical historical behavior. closeTrade now nets fees into pnl/equity; cooldown anchor moved to the closing bar's CLOSE (tfSec derived from min positive bar delta) to match the live wall-clock gate.
- Fixed the LIVE engine for the mirrored blind spot: bars that closed while the process was down (restart/hot-reload/feed gap) were skipped for decisions — a strategy exit firing on a missed bar left the position open until the condition repeated. algo-engine.ts now replays unseen closed bars (cap 30, oldest→newest): exits execute late-at-market (journaled "replayed missed bar"), entries suppressed as stale, per-side signal memory refreshed per bar so the fresh-signal edge gate stays honest.
- Live cooldown gate now arms at eval-bar close instead of raw detection time (backtest parity).
- Route /api/algo/backtest accepts slippageBps/feeBps (clamped 0..500); BacktestPanel got "Slippage (bps)" + "Fee (bps / fill)" inputs and an honest fill-model note (market orders seconds after close, % legs anchor on pre-order quote, missed-bar entries never fire, IEX-vs-SIP paper fills, crypto deep-window source split Alpaca-vs-Binance).
- Verified: scripts-tmp/costmodel-verify.ts — engine cost model $-9575.80 vs probe's independent re-pricing $-9575.95 (15¢ apart, model-anchoring difference); cooldown probe: 30m cooldown → 30 trades, 0 violations. Parity probe after fixes: ALL 4 strategies diffs=0 (bb 9/9, rsi2 52/52, vwap 7/7, orb 1/1).
- E2E (agent-browser, logged in as trader): Backtest panel renders the new inputs; TSLA 5m rsi2 30d with slip 2/fee 10 → NET P&L −$151.18 (−1.51%), frictionless re-run → +$106.29 (+1.06%) — the exact live-vs-backtest gap, now visible/modelable in the UI. dev.log clean, lint clean. (Auth note: browser TOTP flow kept 401ing; injected the curl-obtained session cookie — server-side login verified fine via API.)

Stage Summary:
- DECISION RULES ARE IN PARITY: backtest decide() ≡ live evalStrategy() bar-for-bar on all 4 strategies (probe-verified). The user-perceived discrepancy is EXECUTION: costs (zero-cost backtest vs real fills), fill timing, % legs anchored on quote not fill, missed-bar blindness after restarts (live), IEX-bars-vs-SIP-fills, crypto source split on deep windows.
- Fixed: warmup off-by-one (backtest could start 1 bar late vs live), missed-bar exit replay (live could leave positions stuck), cooldown anchor mismatch.
- New: optional slippageBps/feeBps cost model in engine+route+UI (default 0, frictionless preserved). Probe tools kept in scripts-tmp/ (parity-probe.ts, costmodel-verify.ts, cooldown-verify.ts, parity-debug*.ts).
- Files: src/lib/backtest.ts, src/lib/algo-engine.ts, src/app/api/algo/backtest/route.ts, src/components/terminal/tabs/BacktestPanel.tsx, + probes.

Task ID: 85
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: User question #2 — "the spread that bid/ask price in live VS backtest env": verify whether bid/ask spread is a live-vs-backtest discrepancy source, and make it measurable + modelable.

Work Log:
- Confirmed the gap is REAL: backtest fills at bar prices (last-trade: entry=next open, exit=close, TP=limit, SL=stop+slip) while live entries/exits are MARKET orders that cross the book (buy@ask, sell@bid) — every live round trip pays the full spread, backtest paid 0 by default. The app ingested NO bid/ask anywhere (Quote was last-trade only).
- Data layer (Task 85): Quote type gains optional bid/ask; Binance getBinanceQuotes now merges /api/v3/ticker/bookTicker (parallel per 25-symbol chunk, sanitized uncrossed) onto the 24hr ticker; Alpaca StockSnapshot/CryptoSnapshot parse latestQuote.bp/ap; simQuote synthesizes a tiered spread (1–8bps by price).
- Backtest engine: new spreadBps config — HALF charged adversely per market/stop fill (round trip = full spread), stacking with slippageBps; TP resting limits stay clean. Route parses/clamps spreadBps (0..500).
- Live engine (algo-engine): on entry, when the book is SANE (uncrossed, ≤100bps of mid), % TP/SL legs + Risk sizing anchor on the FILL-SIDE quote (buy→ask, sell→bid) instead of last trade, and the ENTRY journal logs "spread X.Xbps". Wide/stale books (IEX-only NBBO can sit 5% wide off-hours, e.g. TSLA bid 353.96/ask 372.98 vs last 372.21) fall back to last-trade anchoring exactly as before — the sanity gate prevents a stale print from dragging legs 5% away.
- BacktestPanel: new Spread (bps) input (between Slippage and Fee, payload+deps wired); live-spread probe polls /api/market/quote for cfg.symbol every 60s and shows "≈X.X live" chip (≤100bps gate) that seeds the input on click; fill-model note rewritten (spread semantics, fill-side leg anchor, "journal logs the live spread — mirror it here").
- Verified: bun probe — spreadBps 0 vs 20 on 3000 synthetic bars: netPnl −487 → −926, delta ≈ Σmarket-notional × half-spread (463.79 expected vs 438.49 actual, path-dependent leg shift); E2E via agent-browser: BTC/USD 5m 31d, spread=0 → $18,326 / 183.26% / PF 1.52; chip "≈3.3 live" clicked → spread=3 → $10,228 / 102.28% / PF 1.27 — $238/trade expectancy delta ≈ 3bps × $800k notional (qty 10 BTC) as predicted. TSLA hint correctly hidden (5% wide IEX book). No console/page errors, lint clean.

Stage Summary:
- ANSWER to the user: yes — spread was a genuine unmodeled discrepancy (live pays ask/bid every fill; backtest was frictionless at bar prices). Now: real bid/ask ingested (Binance bookTicker + Alpaca latestQuote), backtest Spread (bps) input with a LIVE spread hint chip, live journal logs the spread per entry, legs anchor fill-side when the book is sane.
- Spread semantics: spreadBps = full round-trip cost (half per market fill). For crypto majors expect ~0.5–3bps; large-cap stocks ~1–2bps (SIP); IEX-wide prints are gated out, not trusted.
- Files: src/lib/market-types.ts, src/lib/binance.ts, src/lib/alpaca.ts, src/lib/simulator.ts, src/lib/backtest.ts, src/lib/algo-engine.ts, src/app/api/algo/backtest/route.ts, src/components/terminal/tabs/BacktestPanel.tsx.
- Version stamp v1.13.0 (LandingPage). Commit pending user instruction (push not requested yet).

Task ID: 86
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: User question — "backtest timeframe is 1m/5m/15m, is this solid for backtest?" — audit per-TF soundness, fix what's unsound.

Work Log:
- Audited the per-TF replay model end-to-end: live EOD gate is WALL-CLOCK (algo-engine.ts:404 `nowMinEt >= 15:45` on the 15s tick) while the backtest filled the flatten at the gate bar's CLOSE — exit at 15:46 (1m) / 15:50 (5m) / 16:00 (15m), up to 15 minutes past the live exit and through the close auction. THE unsound bit for coarse TFs.
- FIXED backtest.ts: EOD flatten now fires BEFORE the intrabar bracket check, fills at the gate bar's OPEN (15:45:00 price = the live market order), and preempts that bar's TP/SL (on aligned 1m/5m/15m bars the gate bar STARTS at 15:45 — the whole bar is post-gate, so no bracket fills there; matches live precedence where the wall-clock flatten outranks resting exits). Same-bar entries (signal on the 15:40 bar) now wash at open (entry open → EOD open) with both fills' costs — matches the live enter-then-immediately-flatten net effect. Header + panel fill-model note updated.
- Probe (/tmp/eod-check.ts): synthetic 15m equity bars with late-day ramp → ALL EOD exits at ET-minute 945 exactly, exitPx == gate-bar open (was 16:00 close). Spread + cost-model probes re-run clean (EOD change orthogonal). Lint clean.
- E2E: TSLA 15m 30d via UI → 19 trades, PF 1.47, avg hold 2.7h, 0 errors. dev.log clean.
- Documented (not fixed — both engines share it, parity holds): ORB opening-range pollution on 15m when orMinutes is NOT a multiple of 15 (e.g. 5/10/20: the last OR bar spans up to 14 min past the window) — defaults (15) and 30/45/60 are exact; 5m is exact for all step-5 values; 1m always exact.
- Audit verdict per TF: 1m — most live-faithful fills (next-open ≈ seconds-after-close) but thin stock history depth + costs dominate tight targets; 5m — solid middle ground, all windows clean; 15m — now sound after the EOD fix; ORB range = exactly the 9:30 bar (orMinutes 15), fewer trades = wider stats. TF set itself (1m/5m/15m) is the right intraday envelope: vwap-fade/orb15 are session-anchored strategies that don't exist on 1D; rsi2/bb could theoretically run 1h swing but would need OR window clipping + EOD rework — out of scope.
- Version stamp v1.13.1.

Stage Summary:
- 1m/5m/15m backtest is now SOLID per-TF: the last genuine per-TF divergence (EOD fill timing) is fixed with wall-clock parity; brackets preempted on the gate bar; ORB coarse-TF caveat documented. Files: src/lib/backtest.ts, src/components/terminal/tabs/BacktestPanel.tsx, src/components/landing/LandingPage.tsx.

New sweepStrategies(series, base, {minTrades, topX, maxCombosPerStrategy}): for EVERY strategy in the catalog (1) replay the untouched DEFAULTS (always a candidate even when the optimizer's stride would skip it), (2) run the auto grid through the same budget/stride machinery as the manual optimizer (≤800 sims per strategy, defaults to COMBO_BAR_BUDGET/series.n); dedupe by (strategy, params) signature, rank by the existing Calmar-style score (net% ÷ maxDD%, minTrades gate), return top X (1..20, default 8) with perStrategy stats (grid size, sims, skipped, truncated, scorable count, best score). Empty state when nothing clears minTrades with remediation hints. Sweep survives a strategy switch (it's a cross-strategy artifact — applying a row switches strategy and the list must stay) but clears on symbol/timeframe change (measured on other data).
- APPLY (AlgoTab): onApplyParams extended to (params, strategy?) — a sweep row of a DIFFERENT strategy switches the setup strategy AND normalizes its full param set against that strategy's catalog in one click (previously impossible: params were always merged into the CURRENT strategy). Toast names the newly applied strategy.

- Quickest wins: raise barsFor TTL ≥ poll cadence (or move polls to SSE), persist bars in SQLite with tail refresh, run sweeps/backtests in a worker thread with bars-cache entries ≤4, add gzip `encode` in Caddy, next/dynamic for recharts tabs, prod build instead of next dev.

Stage Summary:
- Feature surface is very broad and the per-request reliability engineering is genuinely strong (timeouts, pagination, fallbacks, reason surfacing); the biggest structural risks are operational, not per-request: dev-mode-in-production, in-process-only schedulers that stop with the browser closed or process restart, and no React error boundary.
- Top fixes ranked: (1) add app/error.tsx + global-error.tsx; (2) server-side alert/notify scheduler + boot-time ensureAlgoEngines; (3) set AUTH_SECRET/APP_ENCRYPTION_SECRET and add login rate limiting; (4) swap `next dev` for the standalone build under a process manager with /api/health; (5) cap concurrent backtest/sweep jobs (memory) and silence prisma:query log spam.

- Found hardcoded Alpaca credentials (KEY PKGJ****ZGOR + secret 6fwV****TSe) in scripts-tmp/alpos-flatten.mjs:24 and key ID in 14 more scripts-tmp files — tracked in git and pushed to github.com/iain504/blackhole_terminal (≥5 history commits).
- Found db/custom.db, .env, upload/v24.tar + v23-algo.zip tracked in git (DB holds 3 users' plaintext TOTP secrets; tarball embeds .env + .git).
- Verified in running deployment: AUTH_SECRET/APP_ENCRYPTION_SECRET unset → session HMAC + credential encryption use the public hardcoded default (auth.ts:8, crypt.ts:8) → forgeable session cookies; cookie also lacks `secure`; no middleware.ts, no rate limiting/lockout anywhere.
- Caddyfile XTransformPort (Caddyfile:2-13) = unauthenticated reverse proxy to ANY localhost port (SSRF/port-scan); stream-service socket.io is open with CORS '*' (index.ts:40); market/* routes accept anonymous calls (user?.id fallback); expensive endpoints (backtest sweep, reco refresh 4-min matrix) have no quotas.

Stage Summary:
- CRITICAL: leaked Alpaca paper creds in git (rotate + history purge), forgeable sessions via default AUTH_SECRET (fail-closed + rotate), git-tracked DB/tarball/.env with plaintext TOTP secrets.
- HIGH: env-key fallback lets any registered user trade the shared broker account (close registration, no fallback, server-side mode); XTransformPort loopback SSRF; open CORS-* WS.
- MEDIUM: no rate limiting on login/TOTP + non-constant-time TOTP compare; no quotas on CPU-heavy endpoints; public market-data proxy; missing `secure`/HSTS/CSP headers.
- Verdict: app-layer authz solid, ops/secret layer not production-safe — top fixes ~2h: rotate keys, set env secrets, untrack DB/tar/.env, close registration, pin XTransformPort=3030.

Stage Summary:
- Engine mechanics are unusually sound: no lookahead bias, conservative stop-first intrabar rule, honest cost model (slip+half-spread+fees), DST-correct ET session handling, demo-data blocked, live/backtest decision rules in verified byte-parity.
- Top risks ranked: (1) no margin/buying-power constraint -> fixed-qty crypto net% is a leverage illusion; (2) maxDD is realized-only -> Calmar score denominator understated; (3) crypto live exit emulation (half-position dual legs, stop-limit, long-only) diverges from backtest full-size TP-or-SL; (4) pure in-sample optimizer + rank-1 selection with minTrades 10 -> 11-23-trade "winners" are multiple-testing artifacts (BTC winner sits on the devPct=5 grid boundary).
- Trust: equity backtests with TP/SL brackets and modest qty, the parity layer, and live P&L accounting (broker fills, FIFO) are trustworthy; trust the absolute crypto net%/score numbers and the reco leaderboard ranking NOT AT ALL without re-sizing to notional limits and an out-of-sample re-run.

- INSTRUMENTATION: created src/instrumentation.ts (runtime-agnostic shell: module-level `registered` flag + globalThis __ttInstrumented guard vs double registration; NEXT_RUNTIME==='nodejs' branch, dynamic import of node boot logic — verified via controlled recompiles that the Edge instrumentation bundle no longer traces crypt/auth/prisma; Next 16 needs NO experimental flag, file is auto-detected) + src/instrumentation-node.ts (bootNode(): console.log('[instrumentation] booted'), then for each DISTINCT user with AlgoRun status='running' → ensureAlgoEngines(userId) (algo-engine's resume fn is PER-USER — boot loop fans out over distinct owners; idempotent per run so races with early /api/algo touches are harmless), then startAlertsSupervisor(); every step try/catch console.warn — boot can never crash from this hook).
- ALERTS EVALUATION EXTRACTED: created src/lib/alerts-eval.ts — evaluateAlerts(userId) preserves the route's exact semantics line-for-line: price alarms via getQuotes, RSI/MACD via barsFor grouped by symbol|tf (demo-bar guard, take 100, createdAt asc), trend-line alarms re-project the drawing through wall-clock time, first-check-records-side rule (indicator/trend start lastSide=null; price seeded opposite), same Telegram texts + triggered[] shape. Route src/app/api/alerts/check/route.ts is now a thin requireUser + evaluateAlerts wrapper (response { triggered, checked } unchanged; verified 401 unauth / compiles clean).
- DOUBLE-FIRE SAFETY: firing is a conditional updateMany claim — WHERE { id, status:'active', lastSide: <snapshot side we evaluated against> } SET { status:'triggered', triggeredAt, lastSide:<new side> }; only count>=1 pushes the result + sends Telegram (compare-and-swap on the crossing memory, mirroring strategy.ts armed→placing). Side-recording writes guarded with status:'active' so they can't clobber a triggered row. evaluateAlerts adds a per-user inFlight set (2nd concurrent caller = no-op { triggered: [], checked: 0 }).
- SUPERVISOR: created src/lib/alerts-supervisor.ts — startAlertsSupervisor(): 20s setInterval (unref'd), globalThis __ttAlertsSupervisor handle (HMR-safe, no duplicate intervals), busy flag skips overlapping cycles, cheap db.alert.count({status:'active'}) gate before any work, per-user try/catch inside each cycle, immediate first pass at start (catches alerts that fired while the process was down); stopAlertsSupervisor() for tests/HMR.
- VERIFIED: bun run lint clean; tsc --noEmit — zero errors in the new files (only pre-existing skills//tests/ errors remain); curl /api/health → HTTP 200 {"status":"ok","uptimeSec":3652,"memoryMB":2110,"db":true,"algoRuns":0,"recoAgeMin":56,"stream":"unknown","version":"v1.80.0+104"} (second hit 4ms cached; stream 'unknown' = connection refused — 3030 not listening yet, another agent owns /healthz); 404 page renders on a bogus route; throwaway scripts-tmp boot probe ran bootNode() end-to-end (booted log, supervisor started, first cycle fetched TSLA bars for the 4 active alerts, stop clean) — probe deleted.
- INSTRUMENTATION HOT-LOAD: the long-running dev server did NOT re-invoke register() for the new file (expected — register runs at process boot; touches alone only recompiled). The next server restart (performed by the restart-owning agent; dev.log got truncated by tee) shows the full boot sequence: "✓ Starting... → [instrumentation] booted → [alerts] supervisor started (every 20s) → ✓ Ready in 1020ms" and the supervisor's immediate first cycle evaluated the 4 active alerts with NO browser open ([bars] stock TSLA fetch). Boot hook is live.

Work Log:
- prisma/schema.prisma: added `model Bar { symbol, tf, time Int, open/high/low/close/volume Float, @@id([symbol, tf, time]), @@index([symbol, tf]) }` (time = unix-seconds bar OPEN). `bun run db:push` OK (additive; client regenerated).
- NEW src/lib/bar-store.ts (server-only): saveBars (batched multi-row INSERT OR REPLACE via $executeRawUnsafe, 140 rows/statement = 980 bind params — safe under SQLite's 999 limit everywhere; measured 127k rows/s synthetic, 111,615 real BTC 5m bars in 1.4s), loadBars (range SELECT, time-sorted), hasCoverage (covered = maxTime >= toSec - 2*tfSec && minTime <= fromSec + 2*tfSec, gap-tolerant), deleteBars (test/admin). All DB failures are non-fatal for callers.
- src/lib/alpaca.ts: crypto DEEP path (>9k bars, symbol.includes('/'), no scroll-back) now tries the store first (serveCryptoFromBarStore): on coverage hit it loads [from..now] from SQLite, ALWAYS tail-refreshes (~2d slice, min 7d floor) from Alpaca, upserts the closed tail, merges (tail overlays by time), logs `[bar-store] hit sym tf n`; forming bar never persisted (still forwarded in the response, same as before). On miss it deep-fetches as before then saveBars the complete closed window (`[bar-store] save ... in Xms`). 1W/1M excluded (aggregated from daily — raw bars only); STOCK path 100% untouched (split-adjustment risk).
- Timings (scripts-tmp probe, BTC/USD): 15m 730d cold 32.2s (86,251 bars + 965ms save) → warm 0.82s (≈39×); 5m 365d cold ~20s → warm 1.0–1.6s (tail fetch dominates). Probes deleted after.
- backtest.ts: (1) MARK-TO-MARKET DD — peak/trough now tracked on realized+unrealized equity (bar-close MTM while a position is open, mtmTrack() at every closeTrade + bar end); metrics.maxDrawdown/maxDrawdownPct are MTM; the DISPLAYED equity curve stays realized-only. (2) NOTIONAL CAP — new cfg `maxNotionalX` (default 1): every entry (fixed AND risk sizing) clamped to (capital×maxNotionalX)/fill, 4dp for crypto / whole-share for stocks; clamped position < $10 = dust → skipped (suppressed++); TP/SL brackets derive from the same capped fill. (3) CRYPTO LONG-ONLY — runBacktest drops sell entries when cfg.isCrypto (new metrics.skippedShorts), live engine untouched. (4) WALK-FORWARD — sweepStrategies opts.oosFraction (0..0.5, default 0): sweep runs IN-SAMPLE on bars[0..floor(n·(1−f))), then each top row is re-run untouched on the OOS tail (warmup from OOS start) → row.oos {netPct, profitFactor, winRate, maxDrawdownPct, trades} | null (< 3 OOS trades); row.boundary = any tuned param on its grid min/max (bounds captured per strategy from autoOptimizeRanges via the same dimValues sanitization the optimizer uses). SweepResult gains oosFraction + skippedShorts. Backward compatible: all additive/optional.

Grep: zero Alpaca key IDs/secret left in the working tree (git HISTORY still has them — rotation+purge are user actions).
- src/lib/auth.ts:10-18 fail-closed — module-load throw when AUTH_SECRET missing OR equal to the old public default; signing uses SESSION_SECRET (typed string, tsc-clean). setSessionCookie/clearSessionCookie now read `x-forwarded-proto` (next/headers, first hop) and set `secure` only when https (localhost http unaffected). Added clientIp() helper (x-forwarded-for → x-real-ip → 'unknown').
- src/lib/crypt.ts: key = scrypt(APP_ENCRYPTION_SECRET || AUTH_SECRET) — throws at load if BOTH missing; decrypt() tries the new key first, on GCM tag failure retries the LEGACY key ('zai-tradeterminal-local-dev-secret') so existing encrypted broker keys keep working; encrypt() always uses the new key. Exported signatures unchanged (settings route, user/config route, alpaca.ts, fill-notifier.ts call sites verified).
- src/lib/totp.ts: verifyTotp comparison now constant-time (SHA-256 both sides → crypto.timingSafeEqual, no length leak); added encryptTotpSecret/decryptTotpSecret — store format `enc:<base64(AES-256-GCM payload)>` reusing crypt.ts; legacy plaintext values pass through decryptTotpSecret unchanged (marked SERVER-ONLY).
- TOTP at-rest migration: POST /api/auth/register stores `encryptTotpSecret(body.totpSecret)`; POST /api/auth/login/verify decrypts via decryptTotpSecret() and after a SUCCESSFUL verification re-encrypts + persists legacy plaintext secrets (one-time UPDATE, non-fatal on error). Verified live: 'trader' row migrated from 32-char plaintext to enc:… and decrypt(enc) === original.
- src/lib/rate-limit.ts (NEW): exact API `rateLimit(key, max, windowMs) → {ok, retryAfterSec}` + `resetRateLimit(key)`; in-memory sliding window (Map of timestamps), 60s periodic prune, server-only comment.
- Rate limits applied: PUT+POST /api/auth/register → 5/10min per IP key `reg:<ip>` (PUT success resets the bucket so legit logins never accumulate); POST /api/auth/login/verify → 10/5min per IP+username key `loginv:<ip>:<username>`; /api/auth/2fa/setup GET → 10/10min per IP. All 429s carry a generic message + retry-after header.
- Account lockout (login/verify route): 5 consecutive TOTP failures per username → 15-min in-memory lock (generic 429, retry-after 900), cleared on success; found+fixed a bug during verification (lockedUntil=0 vs expired-window handling made the counter reset every attempt — caught because the 6th attempt with a correct code returned 200; after fix: 401×5 → 429 on 6th even with correct code).
- Registration flow decision: PUT /api/auth/register is LOGIN STEP 1 (password → ticket) and POST is user-CREATION, so ALLOW_REGISTRATION (default false when unset) gates ONLY POST → 403 {"error":"Registration is disabled"}; PUT stays open (login unaffected). Anti-enumeration: existing username during registration now returns generic 400 {"error":"Cannot register"} (was 409 "Username already taken"); verified with ALLOW_REGISTRATION=true temporarily, then reverted.
- src/lib/db.ts: prisma query log gated behind DEBUG_DB=1 (default ['error','warn']); WAL + busy_timeout pragmas executed once per client — NOTE: $executeRawUnsafe rejects PRAGMAs that return rows ("Execute returned results…"), so they run via $queryRawUnsafe with .catch(() => {}) (non-fatal); verified journal_mode=wal + -wal/-shm files appear.
- Restarted dev server with new .env (pkill 'next dev' → setsid nohup bun run dev); /api 200; no boot errors; ran `bun run lint` (clean) and `bunx tsc --noEmit` (src/lib+src/app clean; pre-existing errors only in examples/, scripts-tmp probes, skills/, tests/totp.test.ts which imports functions that never existed in auth.ts).

Stage Summary:
- Fixed: forgeable sessions (AUTH_SECRET fail-closed + fresh random secret — ALL old session cookies are now INVALID, users must log in again); credential encryption keyed by public default (new key + legacy decrypt fallback); TOTP secrets plaintext at rest (new signups encrypted; existing rows migrate transparently on next successful login); non-constant-time TOTP compare; cookie without secure flag; zero rate limiting (register/login/TOTP-setup throttled + TOTP lockout); username enumeration (generic 400); Alpaca key+secret in 15 tracked scripts; .env/db/tarball tracked in git; prisma query spam.
- Orchestrator: commit the staged deletions (.env, db/custom.db, upload/*.tar/*.zip) + everything else; `git add .env.example` (currently untracked by design). Old sessions are invalidated by the AUTH_SECRET change.
- User actions (out of my scope): rotate the Alpaca paper key at the broker (in-history secret stays exposed until rotation makes it dead) + optionally purge git history (BFG/filter-repo) and force-push; the 'trader' test account's TOTP secret is also in git history/scripts-tmp (re-enroll 2FA if that account matters).
- Registration is now CLOSED by default — set ALLOW_REGISTRATION=true in .env to open signups. /api/auth/2fa/setup and register routes are rate-limited; lockout state is per-process (resets on restart) — acceptable for single-instance, use Redis if ever multi-instance.

- For the E2E orchestrator: check chip state transitions (window.__ttStream.status()), the close-confirm dialog flow (order fires ONLY after "Close position"), ticket validation errors appear/disable on keystroke, '?' and the header Keyboard button open the cheatsheet (Esc closes), and the 409 path — noting the server side may land simultaneously from the other agent; this client handles both shapes (with and without `server` payload).

Task ID: 4-b
Agent: chart-perf
Task: ChartPanel perf — scroll-back prepend fast path (no Map+sort per chunk), indicator settings rebuild split (param tweaks no longer recreate series; enabled-SET signature drives lifecycle), IEX partial-tape honesty chip for US equities, forming-bar hot-path array-copy removal. File: src/components/chart/ChartPanel.tsx (NOTE: actual path is src/components/chart/, not terminal/tabs/ — same 4,866-line file the audit referenced).

Work Log:
- SCROLL-BACK FAST PATH (loadOlder, :3137-3168): the old merge built a Map over the ENTIRE history + incoming chunk, sorted it O(n log n), and setData'd the result on every prepend. New: rawFresh is first RTH-filtered into `fresh` (same sessionOf gate as before), then a cheap O(n) strictly-ascending scan runs; if the chunk is ascending (server returns sorted, dup-free bars) AND fresh[last].time < bars[0].time (belt-and-braces — the cursor filter time < cursor ≤ bars[0].time already proves disjointness), merged = [...fresh, ...bars] plain concat with NO Map and NO sort. The old Map/sort merge is kept verbatim as the fallback for out-of-order/duplicate server data (the ascending scan catches internal dupes too since it requires STRICT ascent). Zero RTH-fresh-bars case (fresh.length===0) still produces a same-content copy exactly like the old path; `added`/visible-range restore/markers backfill/volume setData semantics untouched. Verified disjointness proof: rawFresh ⊆ {t < cursor}, cursor = min(oldestRawTime, bars[0].time) → every fresh bar strictly older than every existing bar, so concat IS the merge.
- SETTINGS REBUILD SPLIT — SAFE SUBSET (audit premise partially wrong, documented): settingsVer is NOT bumped by indicator changes — it bumps ONLY on tt:settings-changed (SettingsDialog credential save/test, SettingsDialog.tsx:283) and the chart effect's comment (:3383-3393) + code comment (:384-388) show that full rebuild+refetch is INTENTIONAL ("Refetch market data when credentials change"). Removing settingsVer from the chart effect as literally instructed would silently break credential-change refetch → left it in place and documented it inline. Indicator config changes never touched the chart effect anyway (`ind` was never a chart-effect dep) — the audit's "tweaking an indicator color tears down the whole chart" does not hold in this code; what DID happen: ANY ind identity change (incl. SMA 20→50) re-ran the series lifecycle effect, destroying + recreating every indicator series (removeSeries/addLineSeries churn) then refilling twice. Implemented the split instead: (1) indSetSig (:3412) — stable signature string of ONLY the six enabled flags "vwap|sma|ema|bb|rsi|macd" (param values excluded); (2) series lifecycle effect deps swap `ind` → `indSetSig` (:3478) + reads config via new indRef mirror (:261-265) so pure PARAM tweaks (SMA/EMA length, BB mult, MACD fast/slow/signal) no longer recreate series; (3) data-refill effect (:3485-3491) now refreshes b.settings = ind BEFORE fillIndData on any ind change — critical because fillIndData, updateIndicatorsLast (tick path), strip titles (:770,:776) and indicator-alarm params (:2003,:2018) all read bundle.settings; without the refresh a period tweak would recompute with the stale snapshot; (4) ADDED showExtendedHours to the series effect deps — pre-existing latent bug: an ETH toggle recreates the chart (chart-effect dep) but never re-ran the series effect, leaving indSeriesRef pointing at dead-chart series (indicators dropped after ETH toggle until the next config change); now the bundle is rebuilt on the new chart. Options-only effect (task fix b) NOT added: IndicatorSettings has no color/width/visibility fields (indicators.ts:21-28 — colors are hardcoded at series creation :3435-3457), so there is nothing to applyOptions on a param tweak; the applyOptions pattern at :3425-3433 is strip-margin logic owned by the enabled-SET, which still re-runs where it belongs.
- IEX FEED CHIP (:3722-3734): non-crypto symbols render an `IEX` outline badge right after the symbol button in the chart header (where symbol/TF chips live), wrapped in the shadcn Tooltip (new import :30) with the exact required copy: "Interactive.Exchange feed — real-time but partial tape (~2% of consolidated volume). Prices can deviate from the consolidated tape." isCryptoSymbol(symbol) (contains '/') → no chip (crypto already carries the 7×24 badge; a BINANCE chip was optional and skipped — the 7×24 badge already marks crypto). Header container is pointer-events-none but the chip sits inside the existing pointer-events-auto symbol group, so hover works without enabling canvas clicks.
- FORMING-BAR HOT PATH (:3538-3541 applyTick, :3666-3668 quote-refresh): replaced `barsRef.current = [...bars.slice(0, -1), {...lastB, …}]` (O(n) array copy per coalesced tick / per 10s poll) with in-place last-slot replacement `bars[bars.length - 1] = {...lastB, …}`. barsRef is read imperatively everywhere (grep-verified: no consumer keys on array identity); the coalescing mechanism itself (250ms interval + pendingTickRef) untouched. Side benefit: a loadOlder await racing a tick no longer reverts the tick's close/high/low (the captured array is now the same object being mutated).
- NOT TOUCHED: markers-fetch-after-paint order, tick coalescing path, drawings/overlay renderer, right-click trading, barsRef roll path on new bar (:3522), BacktestPanel/TerminalRoot/PositionsTab/libs/routes/stream-service, dev server. `bars.slice(0,-1)` at :1290 is string truncation in the canvas text wrapper, not a bars copy.
- Verified: bun run lint exit 0; bunx tsc --noEmit clean for src (only pre-existing examples/skills/tests/scripts-tmp noise); re-read full git diff of the file (90 changed lines) against the disjointness/order invariants and all bundle.settings readers.

Stage Summary:
- Scroll-back prepend no longer Map-merges + sorts the ENTIRE history per chunk: O(n log n) serialize → O(n) concat when the (server-guaranteed) strict-order invariant holds, with the old merge as a verified fallback. Big win on deep-crypto histories (100k+ bars) where each prepend used to re-sort everything.
- Indicator PARAM tweaks (period/mult/fast/slow/signal) now refill series data in place — zero series destroy/create, zero network, chart untouched; enabled-SET changes still rebuild the series set; ETH toggle now correctly rebuilds the indicator bundle on the fresh chart (pre-existing drop bug fixed as a side effect of aligning the lifecycles).
- settingsVer intentionally KEPT on the chart effect (credential-change refetch is a feature, not the bug the audit described) — future audits should read the new NB comment at :3388.
- TSLA/AAPL/etc. charts now carry an honest IEX partial-tape chip with tooltip; crypto charts unchanged (7×24 only).
- For the E2E orchestrator: (1) load a chart → scroll back several chunks — bars appear, view doesn't jump, markers extend, no errors in console; (2) tweak an indicator length (e.g. SMA 20→50) — the line re-forms, Network shows NO /api/market/bars or /markers requests, chart does not blank; (3) toggle an indicator off/on — series set rebuilds cleanly; (4) toggle ETH on a stock intraday chart — indicator lines persist (previously they silently dropped); (5) hover the IEX chip on TSLA for the tooltip; BTC/USD shows no IEX chip; (6) change broker keys in Settings → chart DOES fully rebuild+refetch (intentional, preserved).

Work Log:
- NEW src/lib/validation.ts (single home for the 4-d schemas + helpers): zodBadRequest(error) → 400 { error: 'Invalid body', details: issues[0].message }; readJsonSafe(req) → req.json() returning null on malformed JSON (fixes the malformed-JSON-500 class flagged in audit 1-d); SYMBOL_REGEX /^[-A-Za-z0-9./$=:]{1,20}$/ (task regex extended with '-' and ':' — the client's own format guard in terminal.ts:731 accepts `.` `-` `/` `:` (BRK.B / BRK-B / BTC/USD / NYSE:SYM); without them every drawing on such symbols would 400); HEX_COLOR_REGEX /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}$/; boundedScalar = string≤10_000 | number.finite() | boolean.
- SETTINGS (src/app/api/settings/route.ts PUT): order = readJsonSafe (400 on malformed) → settingsBodyTooLarge(raw) JSON.stringify length > 262_144 → 413 { error: 'Payload too large' } → settingsBodySchema.safeParse → zodBadRequest. Schema: z.record(z.string().max(64), settingsV1) where V1..V4 are unrolled unions (scalar | array(scalar).max(500) | record(key≤64, next)) = objects nest max depth 4, arrays hold scalars only, .transform(stripDollarKeys) recursively drops $-prefixed keys (strip discipline — unknown keys are inert because the route hand-picks known fields). Merge semantics byte-identical: existing `s` from DB + the same conditional known-field writes (alpacaKeyId/alpacaSecret/telegramToken/telegramChatId/notifyFills/notifyCloses/tradeMode/tpslPercent) — verified settings JSON in db identical before/after probes.
- DRAWINGS (src/app/api/drawings/route.ts POST + [id]/route.ts PATCH): drawingCreateSchema = { symbol: symbolSchema, kind: z.enum(['hline','vline','trend','fib','rect','text']) (exact DrawingKind from src/store/terminal.ts — there is NO 'clone' kind in this client), points: array 1..500 of { time: number|null optional, price: number.finite, pane: 'price'|'vol'|'rsi'|'macd' optional } (real client shape incl. time:null hlines + pane-scaled points), color: hex optional, width: int 1..10 optional, meta: record(key≤64, scalar) with ≤20 keys refine, nullable+optional }. drawingPatchSchema = same fields all-optional. PATCH keeps exact apply semantics (points replace, meta !== undefined → null clears); old manual 'symbol, kind, points required' 400 replaced by the schema 400.
- WATCHLISTS (src/app/api/watchlists/route.ts POST + [id]/route.ts PATCH): name z.string().trim().min(1).max(60) (route keeps the pre-existing 40-char storage slice), color hex optional (default '#22c55e' unchanged), sortOrder int optional, items = z.array(z.string().max(32)).max(200) — NOTE the REAL client item shape is PLAIN STRINGS (symbols + '#Group' separators), not {symbol,note} objects; the route's existing normalize (uppercase, '#' case-preserving, dedupe, slice 50) untouched.
- LAYOUT (src/app/api/layout/route.ts PUT; POST/sendBeacon delegates): layoutPutSchema = { data: unknown, baseUpdatedAt?: string }. Contract: baseUpdatedAt PROVIDED → Date.parse fail → 400 'Invalid baseUpdatedAt'; row exists AND row.updatedAt.getTime() > baseMs + 2000 (2s clock skew) → 409 { error: 'layout_conflict', server: { updatedAt: row.updatedAt.toISOString(), data: row.data } } (server copy incl. full layout blob so the client can reload); baseUpdatedAt ABSENT → exactly the old last-writer-wins upsert. Success response is now { ok: true, updatedAt: <ISO> } — updatedAt is ADDITIVE (old clients ignore it). No row + baseUpdatedAt → no conflict → upsert (first save can't conflict).
- HEATMAP dead-feed: server (src/app/api/market/heatmap/route.ts) missing quotes now return { symbol, pct: null, price: null } instead of 0/0 (successful entries unchanged; helper tileFor shared by US + crypto groups). market-types.ts HeatSymbol.pct/price → number | null (only consumer is the heatmap route). Client HeatmapTab.tsx: HeatTile pct/price nullable; formatPct/formatPrice return '–' for null; tileBackground(null) = muted zinc rgba(113,113,122,0.15) (no green/red tint); heatWeight(null) = 0.08 floor so dead feeds collapse to an equal grid; tile tooltip 'SYM · no data — click to chart', aria-label 'SYM no data', pct/price spans render '–' in muted zinc text. Tiles stay clickable.
- Not touched: api-helpers.ts readJson<T> (left for legacy routes, per instructions), TerminalRoot.tsx, ChartPanel.tsx, backtest/algo libs, stream-service. No dev-server restart; no build.
- VERIFIED (live curl on :3000 with a minted tt_session cookie for user 'trader' — HMAC userId.expiry sig per auth.ts, real userId from prisma; throwaway scripts deleted after): PUT /api/settings 300KB body → 413 Payload too large; 65-char key / depth-5 object / 11k-char string / 501-array → 400 with first-issue details; {"$gt":1,"$notifyFills":false,"unknownKey":{...},"notifyCloses":true} → 200 (only notifyCloses merged, $+unknown stripped); malformed JSON → 400 (was 500). POST /api/drawings symbol "<script>" → 400 { error:'Invalid body', details:'Invalid symbol' }; kind 'clone' → 400 (enum message); color 'red' → 400; width 11 → 400; 501 points → 400; valid BTC/USD trend (2 pts, pane, meta) → 200 then PATCH 200 then DELETE 200 (test row cleaned). Watchlists: 61-char name → 400; color 'green' → 400; PATCH 201 items → 400 (no mutation); throwaway list PATCH {btc/usd,#Tech,btc/usd,aapl} → items ['BTC/USD','#Tech','AAPL'] (dedupe+sep intact) then deleted. Layout: PUT w/o baseUpdatedAt → 200 {ok,updatedAt}; base = updatedAt−60s → 409 layout_conflict with server.updatedAt+server.data; base = updatedAt (≤2s skew) → 200; baseUpdatedAt 'not-a-date' → 400; no cookie → 401. Heatmap GET → 200, 82 tiles, 0 null / 0 zero-pct (feed currently HEALTHY — null path exercised by types+code only, see below). bun run lint exit 0; bunx tsc --noEmit zero errors in scope.

Settings verified byte-identical pre/post probes. Test drawing + throwaway watchlist deleted; minted cookie never written to the repo; scripts-tmp probes deleted.

Stage Summary:
- All 5 in-scope write routes now parse-then-validate: settings PUT (bounded JSON, 256KB→413, $-key strip), drawings POST/PATCH (symbol charset, 6-kind enum, {time,price,pane} points ≤500, hex color, width 1..10, meta ≤20 keys), watchlists POST/PATCH (name 1..60 trimmed, hex color, ≤200 string items), layout PUT (baseUpdatedAt 409 gate) — 400s are { error: 'Invalid body', details: firstIssueMessage }; malformed JSON bodies now 400 instead of 500.
- LAYOUT 409 CONTRACT for the client agent: send { data, baseUpdatedAt: <ISO from the last successful save's response.updatedAt or GET row updatedAt> }; server returns 409 { error: 'layout_conflict', server: { updatedAt, data } } when the stored row is >2s newer than base; on 409 the client should reload server.data (discard local) or re-apply on top; without baseUpdatedAt nothing changes (open old tabs + beforeunload sendBeacon stay last-writer-wins). NOTE for the client: success responses now carry updatedAt — seed baseUpdatedAt from it; if the beforeunload POST also sends baseUpdatedAt it can 409 (harmless: the 5s autosave already persisted), but simplest is to omit baseUpdatedAt on the beacon flush.
